import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { classifyActionSafety } from '../src/lib/safety/interceptor';
import { SkillStore, SiteSkill, compileTraceToSkill } from '../src/lib/skills/skillStore';
import { AgentStateMachine } from '../src/lib/agent/stateMachine';
import { LLMRouter } from '../src/lib/llm/router';

describe('M4: Skills Cache & Human-in-the-Loop Safety Interceptor Suite', () => {
  describe('Destructive Action Safety Interceptor', () => {
    it('detects financial actions with critical severity', () => {
      const result = classifyActionSafety({ type: 'click', id: 5 }, 'Place Order $129.99', 'button');
      expect(result.isDestructive).toBe(true);
      expect(result.severity).toBe('critical');
      expect(result.reason).toContain('Financial transaction');
    });

    it('detects deletion actions with high severity', () => {
      const result = classifyActionSafety({ type: 'click', id: 8 }, 'Delete Repository', 'button');
      expect(result.isDestructive).toBe(true);
      expect(result.severity).toBe('high');
      expect(result.reason).toContain('Destructive deletion');
    });

    it('detects external communication actions with medium severity', () => {
      const result = classifyActionSafety({ type: 'click', id: 2 }, 'Send Email to Client', 'button');
      expect(result.isDestructive).toBe(true);
      expect(result.severity).toBe('medium');
      expect(result.reason).toContain('External communication');
    });

    it('detects generic submit button actions with low severity', () => {
      const result = classifyActionSafety({ type: 'click', id: 14 }, 'Submit Application', 'submit');
      expect(result.isDestructive).toBe(true);
      expect(result.severity).toBe('low');
    });

    it('allows non-destructive reading, browsing and scrolling actions freely', () => {
      const r1 = classifyActionSafety({ type: 'click', id: 1 }, 'Next Page >', 'link');
      expect(r1.isDestructive).toBe(false);

      const r2 = classifyActionSafety({ type: 'fill', id: 2, value: 'wireless keyboard' }, 'Search box', 'text');
      expect(r2.isDestructive).toBe(false);

      const r3 = classifyActionSafety({ type: 'scroll', value: 'down' });
      expect(r3.isDestructive).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe('IndexedDB 0-Token Skill Store', () => {
    let skillStore: SkillStore;

    beforeEach(() => {
      skillStore = new SkillStore();
    });

    it('saves and matches deterministic site skill for 0-token execution', async () => {
      const sampleSkill: SiteSkill = {
        id: 'test_skill_gh_search',
        hostname: 'github.com',
        pathnamePattern: '/search*',
        goalPattern: 'search repositories for rust',
        steps: [
          { action: 'fill', selector: 'input[name="q"]', value: 'rust' },
          { action: 'press', value: 'Enter', stop_if_url_changes: true },
        ],
        tokenSavings: 1300,
        timesExecuted: 0,
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        updatedAt: Date.now(),
      };

      await skillStore.saveSkill(sampleSkill);

      // Verify retrieval
      const fetched = await skillStore.getSkill('test_skill_gh_search');
      expect(fetched?.hostname).toBe('github.com');
      expect(fetched?.steps.length).toBe(2);

      // Verify matching on identical domain and goal keywords
      const match = await skillStore.findMatchingSkill(
        'https://github.com/search?q=test',
        'Please search repositories for rust projects'
      );
      expect(match).not.toBeNull();
      expect(match?.id).toBe('test_skill_gh_search');

      // Verify record execution
      await skillStore.recordSkillExecution('test_skill_gh_search');
      const updated = await skillStore.getSkill('test_skill_gh_search');
      expect(updated?.timesExecuted).toBe(1);

      // Negative check: different domain does not match
      const noMatch = await skillStore.findMatchingSkill(
        'https://gitlab.com/search',
        'search repositories for rust'
      );
      expect(noMatch).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('State Machine Safety Interceptor Integration', () => {
    it('pauses and requests approval when destructive action is planned', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({
        apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' },
      });

      // Mock LLM to emit a destructive delete action
      vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockResolvedValue({
        response: {
          thought: 'Deleting the selected user record',
          actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 99 }],
          complete: false,
        },
        activeTier: 1,
        provider: 'groq',
      });

      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router, {
        isDestructiveAction: (action) => {
          if (action.id === 99) {
            return { destructive: true, reason: 'Attempting to delete record #99' };
          }
          return { destructive: false };
        },
      });

      // Start task
      await stateMachine.startTask('Delete inactive user', 1);

      const state = stateMachine.getState();
      expect(state.status).toBe('waiting_approval');
      expect(state.requiresApproval).toBe(true);
      expect(state.approvalDetails?.reason).toContain('Attempting to delete record #99');

      // Reject approval
      await stateMachine.approvePendingAction(false);
      expect(stateMachine.getState().status).toBe('paused');
      expect(stateMachine.getState().requiresApproval).toBe(false);
    });

    it('compileTraceToSkill preserves selector on compiled steps', async () => {
      const traces = [
        {
          action: { type: 'click' as const, id: 1 },
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          success: true,
          selector: 'button#submit-btn',
          elementText: 'Submit',
        },
        {
          action: { type: 'fill' as const, id: 2, value: 'test query' },
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          success: true,
          selector: 'input[name="q"]',
          elementText: '',
        },
      ];

      const skill = await compileTraceToSkill('https://example.com/search', 'search query', traces);
      expect(skill.steps.length).toBe(2);
      expect(skill.steps[0].selector).toBe('button#submit-btn');
      expect(skill.steps[1].selector).toBe('input[name="q"]');
    });
  });
});

# AGENTS.md — Vision AI Development & Loop Architecture

## System Overview
Vision AI is an ultra-low-token, multi-LLM autonomous Chrome agent browser extension built on Manifest V3 with WXT, React 19, TypeScript (strict), and Tailwind CSS.

## Architecture Guidelines
1. **Byte-Stable Contexts:** All LLM conversation prefixes must remain 100% byte-stable across conversational turns to maximize Anthropic and Gemini prompt cache hit rates (>90% cache discount).
2. **DOM Distillation Budget:** Viewport DOM must be distilled to under 800 tokens using landmark-based filtering and numeric integer tags `[id]`.
3. **Speculative Macro-Batching:** Multi-action execution with localized validation and early abort triggers on DOM mutations or URL navigation.
4. **Hardware CDP Actuation:** Mouse & keyboard actions are dispatched via `chrome.debugger` (`Input.dispatchMouseEvent` / `Input.dispatchKeyEvent`) with `event.isTrusted === true`, with seamless fallback to synthetic events.
5. **Deterministic Skill Cache:** Zero-token re-execution of recorded domain workflows compiled to resilient CSS/ARIA selectors in IndexedDB.

## Quality & Verification Gates
- **Gate A (Type Safety):** `npm run typecheck` (`tsc --noEmit`) with 0 errors and no `any` types.
- **Gate B (Unit & Budget Tests):** `npm run test` (`vitest run`) with all tests passing.
- **Gate C (MV3 Extension Build):** `npm run build` (`wxt build`) outputting `.output/chrome-mv3`.
- **Gate D (Browser Artifact):** Live browser verification for DOM and UI components.

File: src/lib/llm/adapters/groq.ts

import {
  LLMProvider,
  LLMProviderType,
  ChatMessage,
  LLMRequestOptions,
  AgentPlanResponse,
  UsageLimitError,
} from '../types';
import { parseAgentPlanResponse } from '../parser';

interface OpenAIChatResponse {
  choices?: Array<{
    message?: {
      content?: string;
    };
  }>;
  usage?: {
    prompt_tokens?: number;
    completion_tokens?: number;
    total_tokens?: number;
  };
  error?: {
    message?: string;
  };
}

export class GroqAdapter implements LLMProvider {
  readonly providerType: LLMProviderType = 'groq';
  private apiKey: string;
  private defaultModel: string;

  constructor(apiKey: string, defaultModel = 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile') {
    this.apiKey = apiKey;
    this.defaultModel = defaultModel;
  }

  async chat(messages: ChatMessage[], options?: LLMRequestOptions): Promise<AgentPlanResponse> {
    const rawText = await this.rawChat(messages, options);
    const parsed = parseAgentPlanResponse(rawText);
    return parsed;
  }

  async rawChat(messages: ChatMessage[], options?: LLMRequestOptions): Promise<string> {
    if (!this.apiKey) {
      throw new Error('Groq API key is not configured.');
    }

    const model = options?.model ?? this.defaultModel;

    const formattedMessages = messages.map((m) => {
      let contentStr = '';
      if (typeof m.content === 'string') {
        contentStr = m.content;
      } else {
        contentStr = m.content
          .map((c) => (c.type === 'text' ? c.text ?? '' : ''))
          .join('\n');
      }
      return {
        role: m.role,
        content: contentStr,
      };
    });

    const response = await fetch('https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'content-type': 'application/json',
        Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model,
        messages: formattedMessages,
        temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.1,
        max_tokens: options?.maxTokens ?? 1024,
        response_format: { type: 'json_object' },
      }),
      signal: options?.signal,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errText = await response.text();
      const isLimitOrUnavailable =
        response.status === 429 ||
        response.status === 402 ||
        response.status === 404 ||
        response.status === 502 ||
        response.status === 503 ||
        /rate limit|rate_limit|quota|token limit|tpm|rpm|decommissioned|not found|does not exist|unavailable/i.test(errText);
      if (isLimitOrUnavailable) {
        throw new UsageLimitError(
          `Groq model unavailable or rate limit exceeded (${response.status}): ${errText}`,
          'groq',
          model,
          response.status
        );
      }
      throw new Error(`Groq API error (${response.status}): ${errText}`);
    }

    const data = (await response.json()) as OpenAIChatResponse;
    if (data.error) {
      const msg = data.error.message ?? 'Unknown';
      if (/rate limit|rate_limit|quota|token limit|tpm|rpm|decommissioned|not found|does not exist|unavailable/i.test(msg)) {
        throw new UsageLimitError(`Groq model error: ${msg}`, 'groq', model);
      }
      throw new Error(`Groq error: ${msg}`);
    }

    const text = data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? '';
    return text;
  }
}

import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { parseAgentPlanResponse } from '../src/lib/llm/parser';
import { AgentStateMachine } from '../src/lib/agent/stateMachine';
import { LLMRouter } from '../src/lib/llm/router';
import { OpenRouterAdapter } from '../src/lib/llm/adapters/openrouter';
import { UsageLimitError } from '../src/lib/llm/types';

describe('Vision AI Smooth Agent Loop & Resilient Parser Suite', () => {
  describe('Resilient Plan Parser', () => {
    it('parses boolean complete: true', () => {
      const json = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'Task completed successfully',
        actions: [],
        complete: true,
      });
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(json);
      expect(plan.complete).toBe(true);
      expect(plan.actions).toEqual([]);
      expect(plan.thought).toBe('Task completed successfully');
    });

    it('parses string complete: "true" gracefully', () => {
      const json = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'Done with steps',
        actions: [],
        complete: 'true',
      });
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(json);
      expect(plan.complete).toBe(true);
    });

    it('parses alternate completion flags like done: true and status: "completed"', () => {
      const json1 = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'All done',
        actions: [],
        done: true,
      });
      expect(parseAgentPlanResponse(json1).complete).toBe(true);

      const json2 = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'Finished all requests',
        actions: [],
        status: 'completed',
      });
      expect(parseAgentPlanResponse(json2).complete).toBe(true);
    });

    it('automatically marks complete when 0 actions provided and thought explicitly indicates goal completed', () => {
      const json = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'I have found the answer: The price is $29.99. Task complete.',
        actions: [],
        complete: false,
      });
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(json);
      expect(plan.complete).toBe(true);
    });

    it('handles numeric IDs passed as string and normalizes action type casing', () => {
      const json = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'Clicking submit',
        actions: [{ type: 'CLICK', id: '42' }],
        complete: false,
      });
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(json);
      expect(plan.actions).toEqual([{ type: 'click', id: 42 }]);
    });

    it('gracefully handles plain text "User Safety" responses without JSON parsing error', () => {
      const raw = 'User Safety: I cannot perform actions that modify browser security settings without confirmation.';
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(raw);
      expect(plan.complete).toBe(true);
      expect(plan.actions).toEqual([]);
      expect(plan.thought).toBe(raw);
    });

    it('extracts JSON when preceded by commentary or markdown blocks', () => {
      const raw = `User Safety Review complete.
\`\`\`json
{
  "thought": "Verified page safety. Clicking next.",
  "actions": [{ "type": "click", "id": 5 }],
  "complete": false
}
\`\`\``;
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(raw);
      expect(plan.thought).toBe('Verified page safety. Clicking next.');
      expect(plan.actions).toEqual([{ type: 'click', id: 5 }]);
      expect(plan.complete).toBe(false);
    });

    it('repairs trailing commas and unescaped syntax flaws in model JSON', () => {
      const raw = `{
        "thought": "All checks passed",
        "actions": [
          { "type": "scroll", "value": "down" },
        ],
        "complete": false,
      }`;
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(raw);
      expect(plan.thought).toBe('All checks passed');
      expect(plan.actions).toEqual([{ type: 'scroll', value: 'down' }]);
      expect(plan.complete).toBe(false);
    });

    it('parses navigate and go_back actions', () => {
      const json = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'Navigating to website and returning',
        actions: [
          { type: 'navigate', value: 'https://github.com' },
          { type: 'go_back' },
        ],
        complete: false,
      });
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(json);
      expect(plan.actions).toEqual([
        { type: 'navigate', value: 'https://github.com' },
        { type: 'go_back' },
      ]);
    });

    it('parses open_tab and switch_tab actions', () => {
      const json = JSON.stringify({
        thought: 'Opening new tab and switching',
        actions: [
          { type: 'open_tab', value: 'https://coursera.org' },
          { type: 'switch_tab', value: 'coursera' },
        ],
        complete: false,
      });
      const plan = parseAgentPlanResponse(json);
      expect(plan.actions).toEqual([
        { type: 'open_tab', value: 'https://coursera.org' },
        { type: 'switch_tab', value: 'coursera' },
      ]);
    });
  });

  describe('Agent State Machine Loop & Anti-Stall Mechanisms', () => {
    it('auto-completes gracefully when model outputs 0 actions with completion thought', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockResolvedValue({
        response: {
          thought: 'The Wikipedia article states that Albert Einstein was born in 1879. Goal completed.',
          actions: [],
          complete: false,
        },
        activeTier: 1,
        provider: 'groq',
      });

      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router);
      await stateMachine.startTask('Find birth year of Einstein', 1);

      const state = stateMachine.getState();
      expect(state.status).toBe('completed');
      expect(state.stepNumber).toBe(1);
    });

    it('breaks infinite loop when identical action is repeated 3 times without progress', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      // Model keeps outputting the exact same click action
      vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockResolvedValue({
        response: {
          thought: 'Clicking button to open menu',
          actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 7 }],
          complete: false,
        },
        activeTier: 1,
        provider: 'groq',
      });

      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router);
      await stateMachine.startTask('Open settings menu', 1);

      const state = stateMachine.getState();
      expect(state.status).toBe('completed');
      expect(state.stepNumber).toBeLessThanOrEqual(5);
      expect(state.currentThought).toContain('loop breaker');
    });

    it('Step 1 Action-First Guardrail intercepts chatbot refusal on course goal and forces browser action', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      // First call: model attempts conversational refusal
      // Second call (after nudge): model provides concrete browser action
      vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep')
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({
          response: {
            thought:
              'Completing an entire CCNA course requires completing multiple modules. Automatically marking or bypassing the course requirements is not possible.',
            actions: [],
            complete: true,
          },
          activeTier: 1,
          provider: 'groq',
        })
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({
          response: {
            thought: 'Clicking Module 1 Introduction to start the course.',
            actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 4 }],
            complete: false,
          },
          activeTier: 1,
          provider: 'groq',
        })
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({
          response: {
            thought: 'Module 1 opened. First section complete.',
            actions: [],
            complete: true,
          },
          activeTier: 1,
          provider: 'groq',
        });

      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router);
      await stateMachine.startTask('complete this course', 1);

      const state = stateMachine.getState();
      expect(state.status).toBe('completed');
      expect(state.completedTraces.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
      expect(state.completedTraces[0].action.type).toBe('click');
      expect(state.completedTraces[0].action.id).toBe(4);
    });

    it('Refusal thoughts with 0 actions are never marked as completed', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockResolvedValue({
        response: {
          thought: 'I cannot bypass course requirements as an AI policy.',
          actions: [],
          complete: true,
        },
        activeTier: 1,
        provider: 'groq',
      });

      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router);
      await stateMachine.startTask('complete this course', 1);

      const state = stateMachine.getState();
      expect(state.status).not.toBe('completed');
    });

    it('allows Step 1 completion when plan.complete is true with informative thought and no refusal', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockResolvedValue({
        response: {
          thought: 'The current page displays the project documentation overview and installation guide.',
          actions: [],
          complete: true,
        },
        activeTier: 1,
        provider: 'groq',
      });

      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router);
      await stateMachine.startTask('Summarize this documentation page', 1);

      const state = stateMachine.getState();
      expect(state.status).toBe('completed');
      expect(state.currentThought).toContain('project documentation overview');
    });

    it('executes navigate and go_back actions and records traces', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockResolvedValue({
        response: {
          thought: 'Navigating to external docs',
          actions: [
            { type: 'navigate', value: 'https://github.com/trending' },
            { type: 'go_back' },
          ],
          complete: true,
        },
        activeTier: 1,
        provider: 'groq',
      });

      const updateSpy = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({});
      const goBackSpy = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({});
      (globalThis as any).chrome = {
        ...(globalThis as any).chrome,
        tabs: {
          query: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue([{ id: 1 }]),
          get: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: 1, url: 'https://github.com/trending', status: 'complete' }),
          update: updateSpy,
          goBack: goBackSpy,
          sendMessage: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ text: 'PAGE: GitHub Trending' }),
        },
      };

      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router);
      await stateMachine.startTask('Check trending repos', 1);

      expect(updateSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1, { url: 'https://github.com/trending' });
      const state = stateMachine.getState();
      expect(state.completedTraces.some((t) => t.action.type === 'navigate')).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe('OpenRouter HTTP 400 Retry & Resilience', () => {
    it('retries once without response_format on HTTP 400 and succeeds if retry returns 200', async () => {
      const adapter = new OpenRouterAdapter('test-key', 'free/model');
      let callCount = 0;
      let secondCallHadResponseFormat = true;

      vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockImplementation(async (_url, init: any) => {
        callCount++;
        const parsedBody = JSON.parse(init.body);
        if (callCount === 1) {
          expect(parsedBody.response_format).toBeDefined();
          return {
            ok: false,
            status: 400,
            text: async () => 'Provider does not support response_format',
          } as Response;
        } else {
          secondCallHadResponseFormat = parsedBody.response_format !== undefined;
          return {
            ok: true,
            status: 200,
            json: async () => ({
              choices: [
                {
                  message: {
                    content: JSON.stringify({
                      thought: 'Successfully retrieved after 400 retry',
                      actions: [],
                      complete: true,
                    }),
                  },
                },
              ],
            }),
          } as Response;
        }
      });

      const res = await adapter.chat([{ role: 'user', content: 'test' }]);
      expect(callCount).toBe(2);
      expect(secondCallHadResponseFormat).toBe(false);
      expect(res.thought).toBe('Successfully retrieved after 400 retry');
      expect(res.complete).toBe(true);
    });

    it('throws UsageLimitError if retry also fails with HTTP 400', async () => {
      const adapter = new OpenRouterAdapter('test-key', 'broken/model');

      vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 400,
        text: async () => 'Invalid model request or unsupported parameter',
      } as Response);

      await expect(adapter.chat([{ role: 'user', content: 'test' }])).rejects.toThrow(UsageLimitError);
    });
  });

  describe('Milestone 7.3: Completion Heuristics & NetAcad SCORM Cross-Frame Execution', () => {
    it('strict completion heuristic guards against future-intent and button labels', () => {
      const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: {} }));
      const hasCompletion = (thought: string) =>
        (stateMachine as any).hasCompletionIndication(thought);

      // Negative cases: thoughts containing future intent or incomplete status
      expect(hasCompletion('I will click Mark as complete next')).toBe(false);
      expect(hasCompletion("Let's click complete")).toBe(false);
      expect(hasCompletion('Clicking Mark as complete button')).toBe(false);
      expect(hasCompletion('Need to complete the remaining quizzes')).toBe(false);
      expect(hasCompletion('Going to open lesson 2')).toBe(false);
      expect(hasCompletion('not done yet')).toBe(false);
      expect(hasCompletion('Scrolling down to find next section')).toBe(false);

      // Positive cases: explicit completion statements without future intent
      expect(hasCompletion('Task is complete.')).toBe(true);
      expect(hasCompletion('The goal is achieved.')).toBe(true);
      expect(hasCompletion('All requested steps are done.')).toBe(true);
      expect(hasCompletion('Successfully marked as complete and finished.')).toBe(true);
      expect(hasCompletion('The answer is 42.')).toBe(true);
    });

    it('cross-frame distillation captures SCORM child frames with offset IDs and targets frameId during execution', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      const fakeChrome: any = {
        tabs: {
          get: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ id: 10, url: 'https://netacad.com/course/module1', status: 'complete' }),
          sendMessage: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
            text: 'PAGE: Cisco NetAcad [https://netacad.com]\n[1] <button>Menu</button>',
          }),
        },
        scripting: {
          executeScript: vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (opts: any) => {
            if (opts.target?.allFrames) {
              // Return child SCORM frame
              return [
                {
                  frameId: 3,
                  result: {
                    frameTitle: 'SCORM Interactive Player',
                    frameUrl: 'https://content.netacad.com/scorm/player.html',
                    items: [
                      {
                        localId: 1,
                        tag: 'button',
                        attrStr: 'type="button"',
                        text: 'Mark as complete',
                      },
                      {
                        localId: 2,
                        tag: 'video',
                        attrStr: '',
                        text: '',
                      },
                    ],
                  },
                },
              ];
            } else if (opts.target?.frameIds?.includes(3)) {
              // Mock execution inside frame 3
              return [{ result: { success: true } }];
            }
            return [{ result: { success: true } }];
          }),
        },
      };

      const originalChrome = (globalThis as any).chrome;
      (globalThis as any).chrome = fakeChrome;

      try {
        let initialPromptObservation = '';
        vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockImplementation(async (messages: any) => {
          initialPromptObservation = messages.map((m: any) => m.content).join('\n');
          return {
            response: {
              thought: 'Clicking Mark as complete in SCORM child player.',
              actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 1001 }],
              complete: true,
            },
            activeTier: 1,
            provider: 'groq',
          };
        });

        const stateMachine = new AgentStateMachine(router);
        await stateMachine.startTask('Complete NetAcad lesson', 10);

        // Verify child frame elements were captured with frame offset (1001, 1002)
        expect(initialPromptObservation).toContain('--- IFRAME [Frame 3: SCORM Interactive Player] ---');
        expect(initialPromptObservation).toContain('[1001] <button type="button">Mark as complete</button>');
        expect(initialPromptObservation).toContain('[1002] <video />');

        // Verify child frame action was executed targeting frameIds: [3]
        expect(fakeChrome.scripting.executeScript).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
          expect.objectContaining({
            target: { tabId: 10, frameIds: [3] },
            args: [{ type: 'click', id: 1 }],
          })
        );
      } finally {
        (globalThis as any).chrome = originalChrome;
      }
    });

    it('isolates state, goals, step counters, and storage keys across multiple concurrent tab agents', async () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({ apiKeys: { groq: 'test-key' } });

      const sessionStorageData: Record<string, any> = {};
      const fakeChrome: any = {
        tabs: {
          get: vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (id: number) => ({
            id,
            url: id === 101 ? 'https://coursera.org/learn/algo' : 'https://netacad.com/courses/ccna',
            status: 'complete',
          })),
          sendMessage: vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (id: number) => ({
            text: id === 101 ? 'PAGE: Coursera\n[1] <button>Start</button>' : 'PAGE: NetAcad\n[1] <button>Resume</button>',
          })),
        },
        storage: {
          session: {
            get: vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (key: string) => ({ [key]: sessionStorageData[key] })),
            set: vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (obj: Record<string, any>) => {
              Object.assign(sessionStorageData, obj);
            }),
          },
        },
      };

      const originalChrome = (globalThis as any).chrome;
      (globalThis as any).chrome = fakeChrome;

      try {
        let stepCount101 = 0;
        let stepCount202 = 0;
        vi.spyOn(router, 'executeStep').mockImplementation(async (messages: any) => {
          const userMsg = messages.find((m: any) => m.role === 'user')?.content || '';
          if (userMsg.includes('Coursera')) {
            stepCount101++;
            if (stepCount101 === 1) {
              return {
                response: {
                  thought: 'Clicking start on Coursera lesson',
                  actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 1 }],
                  complete: false,
                },
                activeTier: 1,
                provider: 'groq',
              };
            } else {
              return {
                response: {
                  thought: 'Coursera Algorithms task is complete.',
                  actions: [],
                  complete: true,
                },
                activeTier: 1,
                provider: 'groq',
              };
            }
          } else {
            stepCount202++;
            return {
              response: {
                thought: 'NetAcad CCNA goal is achieved.',
                actions: [],
                complete: true,
              },
              activeTier: 1,
              provider: 'groq',
            };
          }
        });

        // Initialize two separate agents bound to tab 101 and tab 202
        const agent101 = new AgentStateMachine(router, {}, 'orbit_agent_session_tab_101');
        const agent202 = new AgentStateMachine(router, {}, 'orbit_agent_session_tab_202');

        await agent101.startTask('Complete Coursera Algorithms', 101);
        await agent202.startTask('Complete NetAcad CCNA', 202);

        const state101 = agent101.getState();
        const state202 = agent202.getState();

        // Verify independent goals and tab bindings
        expect(state101.tabId).toBe(101);
        expect(state101.goal).toBe('Complete Coursera Algorithms');
        expect(state101.status).toBe('completed');
        expect(state101.stepNumber).toBe(2);
        expect(state101.currentThought).toBe('Coursera Algorithms task is complete.');

        expect(state202.tabId).toBe(202);
        expect(state202.goal).toBe('Complete NetAcad CCNA');
        expect(state202.status).toBe('completed');
        expect(state202.stepNumber).toBe(1);
        expect(state202.currentThought).toBe('NetAcad CCNA goal is achieved.');

        // Verify isolated per-tab session persistence in chrome.storage.session
        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_101']).toBeDefined();
        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_101'].tabId).toBe(101);
        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_101'].goal).toBe('Complete Coursera Algorithms');
        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_101'].stepNumber).toBe(2);

        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_202']).toBeDefined();
        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_202'].tabId).toBe(202);
        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_202'].goal).toBe('Complete NetAcad CCNA');
        expect(sessionStorageData['orbit_agent_session_tab_202'].stepNumber).toBe(1);
      } finally {
        (globalThis as any).chrome = originalChrome;
      }
    });
  });
});

import 'fake-indexeddb/auto';

// Global chrome API mock for unit tests
if (typeof globalThis.chrome === 'undefined') {
  const storageLocal: Record<string, any> = {};
  const storageSession: Record<string, any> = {};

  (globalThis as any).chrome = {
    storage: {
      local: {
        get: async (keys?: string | string[] | Record<string, any>) => {
          if (!keys) return { ...storageLocal };
          if (typeof keys === 'string') return { [keys]: storageLocal[keys] };
          if (Array.isArray(keys)) {
            const res: Record<string, any> = {};
            for (const k of keys) res[k] = storageLocal[k];
            return res;
          }
          return { ...storageLocal };
        },
        set: async (items: Record<string, any>) => {
          Object.assign(storageLocal, items);
        },
        remove: async (keys: string | string[]) => {
          const list = Array.isArray(keys) ? keys : [keys];
          for (const k of list) delete storageLocal[k];
        },
        clear: async () => {
          for (const k in storageLocal) delete storageLocal[k];
        },
      },
      session: {
        get: async (keys?: string | string[] | Record<string, any>) => {
          if (!keys) return { ...storageSession };
          if (typeof keys === 'string') return { [keys]: storageSession[keys] };
          if (Array.isArray(keys)) {
            const res: Record<string, any> = {};
            for (const k of keys) res[k] = storageSession[k];
            return res;
          }
          return { ...storageSession };
        },
        set: async (items: Record<string, any>) => {
          Object.assign(storageSession, items);
        },
        remove: async (keys: string | string[]) => {
          const list = Array.isArray(keys) ? keys : [keys];
          for (const k of list) delete storageSession[k];
        },
        clear: async () => {
          for (const k in storageSession) delete storageSession[k];
        },
      },
    },
    tabs: {
      get: async (tabId: number) => ({ id: tabId, url: 'https://example.com', title: 'Example Domain' }),
      query: async () => [{ id: 1, url: 'https://example.com', title: 'Example Domain' }],
      sendMessage: async () => ({ success: true }),
      captureVisibleTab: async () => 'data:image/webp;base64,mockWebpData',
      onActivated: { addListener: () => {}, removeListener: () => {} },
      onUpdated: { addListener: () => {}, removeListener: () => {} },
    },
    scripting: {
      executeScript: async () => [{ result: 'distilled DOM text' }],
    },
    debugger: {
      attach: async () => {},
      detach: async () => {},
      sendCommand: async () => ({}),
      onEvent: { addListener: () => {} },
      onDetach: { addListener: () => {} },
    },

import React from 'react';
import { AgentSessionState } from '../../../src/lib/agent/types';
import { Play, Pause, Square, Sparkles, CheckCircle, AlertCircle, Loader2, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';

interface Props {
  state: AgentSessionState;
  prompt: string;
  onPromptChange: (val: string) => void;
  onStart: () => void;
  onPause: () => void;
  onResume: () => void;
  onStop: () => void;
  onReset: () => void;
}

const QUICK_PROMPTS = [
  'Search arXiv for latest autonomous agent papers',
  'Find top trending TypeScript repo on GitHub',
  'Fill checkout form with demo billing address',
];

export function TaskStream({
  state,
  prompt,
  onPromptChange,
  onStart,
  onPause,
  onResume,
  onStop,
  onReset,
}: Props): React.JSX.Element {
  const isRunning = state.status === 'running';
  const isPaused = state.status === 'paused';
  const isWaiting = state.status === 'waiting_approval';
  const isCompleted = state.status === 'completed';
  const isError = state.status === 'error';

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-hidden">
      {/* Prompt Input Form */}
      <div className="bg-orbit-panel border border-orbit-border rounded-xl p-3 shadow-md">
        <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1.5">
          Autonomous Task Goal
        </label>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <textarea
            value={prompt}
            onChange={(e) => onPromptChange(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (prompt.trim() && !isRunning && !isWaiting) {
                  onStart();
                }
              }
            }}
            disabled={isRunning || isWaiting}
            rows={2}
            placeholder="Describe what you want Vision AI to do in this browser tab... (Press Enter to launch)"
            className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-xs text-slate-100 placeholder-slate-500 focus:border-orbit-accent focus:outline-none resize-none font-sans"
          />

          {/* Quick Prompts */}
          {state.status === 'idle' && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {QUICK_PROMPTS.map((qp, i) => (
                <button
                  key={i}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onPromptChange(qp)}
                  className="text-[10px] bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 border border-slate-800 px-2 py-1 rounded-md transition-colors"
                >
                  {qp}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Action Controls */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {isRunning && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onPause}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-600/80 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                >
                  <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Pause
                </button>
              )}
              {isPaused && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onResume}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                >
                  <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-white" /> Resume
                </button>
              )}
              {(isRunning || isPaused || isWaiting) && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onStop}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-rose-900/60 hover:bg-rose-900 text-rose-200 border border-rose-800/80 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                >
                  <Square className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Stop
                </button>
              )}
              {(isCompleted || isError) && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onReset}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white border border-slate-700 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                  title="Clear session and start a new task"
                >
                  <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> New Task
                </button>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Launch / Re-run Button */}
            {!isRunning && !isPaused && !isWaiting && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onStart}
                disabled={!prompt.trim()}
                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-orbit-neon hover:from-emerald-400 hover:to-orbit-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-slate-950 font-bold rounded-lg text-xs shadow-md transition-all ml-auto"
              >
                {isCompleted ? (
                  <>
                    <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Run Next Step
                  </>
                ) : isError ? (
                  <>
                    <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Retry Task
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Launch Vision AI
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Live Step Progression & Thought Stream */}
      <div className="flex-1 bg-orbit-panel border border-orbit-border rounded-xl p-3 shadow-md flex flex-col gap-2.5 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-orbit-border pb-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {isRunning && <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orbit-neon animate-spin" />}
            {isCompleted && <CheckCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />}
            {isError && <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />}
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-200">
              {isRunning
                ? `Step ${state.stepNumber} of ${state.maxSteps}`
                : isCompleted
                ? 'Task Completed Successfully'
                : isError
                ? 'Task Halted'
                : 'Execution Stream'}
            </span>
          </div>
          <span
            className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold uppercase ${
              isRunning
                ? 'bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800 animate-pulse'
                : isWaiting
                ? 'bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800'
                : isPaused
                ? 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border border-slate-700'
                : isCompleted
                ? 'bg-emerald-900/60 text-emerald-200'
                : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-500'
            }`}
          >
            {state.status.replace('_', ' ')}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Auto-Switch Fallback Notice */}
        {state.fallbackNotice && (
          <div className="bg-amber-950/60 border border-amber-700/80 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-xs text-amber-200 flex items-center gap-2 animate-pulse">
            <span className="text-amber-400 font-bold">⚡ Model Failover:</span>
            <span>{state.fallbackNotice}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Completed Result Card */}
        {isCompleted && state.currentThought && (
          <div className="bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-500/50 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs text-emerald-100 flex flex-col gap-1.5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-emerald-400 font-bold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide">
              <CheckCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Final Outcome / Answer</span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-slate-100 font-sans leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap select-text">
              {state.currentThought}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Thought Bubble */}
        {!isCompleted && state.currentThought && (
          <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-xs text-slate-300 italic flex items-start gap-2">
            <span className="text-orbit-accent font-bold not-italic">Thought:</span>
            <span>{state.currentThought}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Error message if any */}
        {state.error && (
          <div className="bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/60 rounded-lg p-2.5 text-xs text-rose-300">
            {state.error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Action Traces Log */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto flex flex-col gap-1.5 pr-1">
          {state.completedTraces.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 text-xs text-center p-4">
              Awaiting task instructions.
            </div>
          ) : (
            state.completedTraces.map((trace, idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className="bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">#{idx + 1}</span>
                  <span className="text-orbit-neon font-bold uppercase">
                    {trace.action.type}
                  </span>
                  {trace.action.id !== undefined && (
                    <span className="text-sky-300">[{trace.action.id}]</span>
                  )}
                  {trace.action.value && (
                    <span className="text-slate-400 truncate max-w-[120px]">
                      &quot;{trace.action.value}&quot;
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                  {trace.success ? '✓ OK' : '✕ ERR'}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { MacroAction, RouterTier, LLMProviderType } from '../llm/types';
import { ActionTrace } from '../executor/macroExecutor';

export type AgentStatus =
  | 'idle'
  | 'running'
  | 'waiting_approval'
  | 'paused'
  | 'completed'
  | 'error';

export interface TokenMetrics {
  totalEstimatedTokens: number;
  cachedPrefixTokens: number;
  freshTokens: number;
  estimatedCostDiscountPercent: number;
}

export interface AgentSessionState {
  status: AgentStatus;
  goal: string;
  tabId: number;
  stepNumber: number;
  maxSteps: number;
  currentThought: string;
  pendingActions: MacroAction[];
  requiresApproval: boolean;
  approvalDetails?: {
    action: MacroAction;
    reason: string;
  };
  tokenMetrics: TokenMetrics;
  completedTraces: ActionTrace[];
  activeProvider?: LLMProviderType;
  activeTier?: RouterTier;
  activeModel?: string;
  fallbackNotice?: string;
  error?: string;
}

export interface TabSessionSummary {
  tabId: number;
  title: string;
  url: string;
  status: AgentStatus;
  stepNumber: number;
  goal: string;
}

export type ExtensionMessage =
  | { type: 'START_TASK'; goal: string; tabId?: number }
  | { type: 'PAUSE_TASK'; tabId?: number }
  | { type: 'RESUME_TASK'; tabId?: number }
  | { type: 'STOP_TASK'; tabId?: number }
  | { type: 'GET_AGENT_STATE'; tabId?: number }
  | { type: 'APPROVE_ACTION'; approved: boolean; tabId?: number }
  | { type: 'LIST_TAB_SESSIONS' }
  | { type: 'DISTILL_VIEWPORT' }
  | { type: 'EXECUTE_MACRO'; actions: MacroAction[] }
  | { type: 'GET_DELTA' }
  | { type: 'REQUEST_VISION_CROP'; targetId: number }
  | { type: 'PING' };

import { MacroAction } from '../llm/types';
import { ElementRegistry, globalElementRegistry, collectAllElementsDeep } from '../dom/distiller';
import { DOMDeltaTracker } from '../dom/delta';
import { CDPActuator } from '../cdp/actuator';

export type AbortReason =
  | 'url_changed'
  | 'target_disappeared'
  | 'alert_or_error_mutated'
  | 'action_failed'
  | 'vision_crop_requested';

export interface ActionTrace {
  action: MacroAction;
  timestamp: number;
  success: boolean;
  error?: string;
  selector?: string;
  elementText?: string;
}

export function highlightTarget(el: HTMLElement): void {
  if (!el || typeof el.style === 'undefined') return;
  const originalOutline = el.style.outline;
  const originalOffset = el.style.outlineOffset;
  el.style.outline = '2px solid #39d353';
  el.style.outlineOffset = '2px';
  setTimeout(() => {
    try {
      el.style.outline = originalOutline;
      el.style.outlineOffset = originalOffset;
    } catch {}
  }, 350);
}

/**
 * Dispatches realistic pointer and mouse event sequence required by modern LMS / SPA UI frameworks
 * (React Aria, MUI, Shoelace, NetAcad Adapt Web Components) with composed: true to cross Shadow DOM boundaries.
 */
export function dispatchRealisticClick(el: HTMLElement): void {
  try {
    el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', inline: 'center', behavior: 'instant' });
  } catch {}

  const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect ? el.getBoundingClientRect() : { left: 0, top: 0, width: 0, height: 0 };
  const clientX = Math.round((rect.left || 0) + (rect.width || 0) / 2);
  const clientY = Math.round((rect.top || 0) + (rect.height || 0) / 2);

  const win = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : undefined;
  const PointerEventClass = typeof PointerEvent !== 'undefined' ? PointerEvent : MouseEvent;

  const baseInit = {
    bubbles: true,
    cancelable: true,
    composed: true,
    clientX,
    clientY,
    button: 0,
    buttons: 1,
    view: win,
  };

  try {
    el.dispatchEvent(new PointerEventClass('pointerover', { ...baseInit, buttons: 0 }));
    el.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseover', { ...baseInit, buttons: 0 }));
    el.dispatchEvent(new PointerEventClass('pointerdown', baseInit));
    el.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mousedown', baseInit));
    if (typeof el.focus === 'function') {
      el.focus();
    }
    el.dispatchEvent(new PointerEventClass('pointerup', { ...baseInit, buttons: 0 }));
    el.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('mouseup', { ...baseInit, buttons: 0 }));
    el.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', { ...baseInit, buttons: 0 }));
    if (typeof el.click === 'function') {
      el.click();
    }
  } catch {
    if (typeof el.click === 'function') {
      el.click();
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Smart scrolling engine:
 * Handles inner overflow-y containers on Coursera and NetAcad (e.g. curriculum sidebars, lecture panes)
 * as well as top-level window scrolling.
 */
export function smartScroll(direction: 'up' | 'down', targetEl?: HTMLElement | null): void {
  const deltaY = direction === 'up' ? -400 : 400;

  if (targetEl) {
    let cur: HTMLElement | null = targetEl;
    let scrollableEl: HTMLElement | null = null;
    while (cur && cur !== document.body && cur !== document.documentElement) {
      if (cur.scrollHeight > cur.clientHeight + 10) {
        scrollableEl = cur;
        break;
      }
      cur = cur.parentElement || ((cur.getRootNode?.() as ShadowRoot)?.host as HTMLElement) || null;
    }

    if (scrollableEl) {
      if (typeof scrollableEl.scrollBy === 'function') {
        scrollableEl.scrollBy({ top: deltaY, behavior: 'instant' });
      } else {
        scrollableEl.scrollTop = (scrollableEl.scrollTop || 0) + deltaY;
      }
      return;
    }
  }

  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      if (typeof window.scrollBy === 'function') {
        window.scrollBy({ top: deltaY, behavior: 'instant' });
      }
    } catch {}

    try {
      const allEls = collectAllElementsDeep(document);
      const scrollableContainers: Array<{ el: HTMLElement; area: number }> = [];

      for (const el of allEls) {
        if (el === document.body || el === document.documentElement) continue;
        if (el.scrollHeight > el.clientHeight + 20) {
          try {
            const style = window.getComputedStyle ? window.getComputedStyle(el) : null;
            const overflowY = style ? style.overflowY || '' : '';
            if (['auto', 'scroll', 'overlay'].includes(overflowY)) {
              const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect ? el.getBoundingClientRect() : { width: 100, height: 100 };
              const width = rect.width || 100;
              const height = rect.height || 100;
              if (width > 20 && height > 20) {
                scrollableContainers.push({ el, area: width * height });
              }
            }
          } catch {}
        }
      }

      scrollableContainers.sort((a, b) => b.area - a.area);

      for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(2, scrollableContainers.length); i++) {
        const container = scrollableContainers[i].el;
        if (typeof container.scrollBy === 'function') {
          container.scrollBy({ top: deltaY, behavior: 'instant' });
        } else {
          container.scrollTop = (container.scrollTop || 0) + deltaY;
        }
      }
    } catch {}
  }
}

export function getElementSelector(el: HTMLElement): string {
  if (el.id) {
    return `#${CSS?.escape ? CSS.escape(el.id) : el.id}`;
  }
  const role = el.getAttribute('role');
  if (role) {
    const name = el.getAttribute('aria-label') || el.getAttribute('name');
    if (name) {
      const esc = CSS?.escape ? CSS.escape(name) : name;
      return `[role="${role}"][aria-label="${esc}"]`;
    }
    return `[role="${role}"]`;
  }
  const name = el.getAttribute('name');
  if (name) {
    const esc = CSS?.escape ? CSS.escape(name) : name;
    return `${el.tagName.toLowerCase()}[name="${esc}"]`;
  }
  const ariaLabel = el.getAttribute('aria-label');
  if (ariaLabel) {
    const esc = CSS?.escape ? CSS.escape(ariaLabel) : ariaLabel;
    return `${el.tagName.toLowerCase()}[aria-label="${esc}"]`;
  }
  const testId = el.getAttribute('data-testid') || el.getAttribute('data-test');
  if (testId) {
    const esc = CSS?.escape ? CSS.escape(testId) : testId;
    return `[data-testid="${esc}"]`;
  }
  if (el.className && typeof el.className === 'string') {
    const classes = el.className.trim().split(/\s+/).filter((c) => !c.includes(':') && !c.includes('/'));
    if (classes.length > 0) {
      return `${el.tagName.toLowerCase()}.${classes.slice(0, 2).map((c) => (CSS?.escape ? CSS.escape(c) : c)).join('.')}`;
    }
  }
  return el.tagName.toLowerCase();
}

export interface MacroExecutionResult {
  totalActions: number;
  executedActions: number;
  abortedEarly: boolean;
  abortReason?: AbortReason;
  executedTraces: ActionTrace[];
}

export interface MacroExecutorOptions {
  registry?: ElementRegistry;
  actuator?: CDPActuator;
  deltaTracker?: DOMDeltaTracker;
  debounceMs?: number;
  getCurrentUrl?: () => string;
}

export class MacroBatchExecutor {
  private registry: ElementRegistry;
  private actuator?: CDPActuator;
  private deltaTracker?: DOMDeltaTracker;
  private debounceMs: number;
  private getCurrentUrl: () => string;

  constructor(options?: MacroExecutorOptions) {
    this.registry = options?.registry ?? globalElementRegistry;
    this.actuator = options?.actuator;
    this.deltaTracker = options?.deltaTracker;
    this.debounceMs = options?.debounceMs ?? 120;
    this.getCurrentUrl =
      options?.getCurrentUrl ??
      (() => (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : ''));
  }

  /**
   * Executes a sequence of speculative macro-actions with mid-batch validation
   * circuit breakers: URL navigation, target node disappearance, and alert/validation error mutations.
   */
  async executeBatch(actions: MacroAction[]): Promise<MacroExecutionResult> {
    const executedTraces: ActionTrace[] = [];
    const initialUrl = this.getCurrentUrl();
    let lastTargetEl: HTMLElement | null = null;

    for (let i = 0; i < actions.length; i++) {
      const action = actions[i];

      // Circuit Breaker 1: URL change when stop_if_url_changes is true
      if (action.stop_if_url_changes && this.getCurrentUrl() !== initialUrl) {
        return {
          totalActions: actions.length,
          executedActions: i,
          abortedEarly: true,
          abortReason: 'url_changed',
          executedTraces,
        };
      }

      // Circuit Breaker 2: Alert or validation error mutated into DOM
      if (this.deltaTracker) {
        const delta = this.deltaTracker.flushDelta();
        if (delta.hasAlertOrError) {
          return {
            totalActions: actions.length,
            executedActions: i,
            abortedEarly: true,
            abortReason: 'alert_or_error_mutated',
            executedTraces,
          };
        }
      }

      // Check Vision Crop action
      if (action.type === 'request_vision_crop') {
        executedTraces.push({
          action,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          success: true,
        });
        return {
          totalActions: actions.length,
          executedActions: i + 1,
          abortedEarly: true,
          abortReason: 'vision_crop_requested',
          executedTraces,
        };
      }

      // Circuit Breaker 3: Target element disappearance
      let targetEl: HTMLElement | null = null;
      if (action.id !== undefined) {
        targetEl = this.registry.getElement(action.id);
        if (!targetEl || !targetEl.isConnected) {
          return {
            totalActions: actions.length,
            executedActions: i,
            abortedEarly: true,
            abortReason: 'target_disappeared',
            executedTraces,
          };
        }
      } else if (action.type === 'press' && lastTargetEl && lastTargetEl.isConnected) {
        targetEl = lastTargetEl;
      }

      if (targetEl) {
        lastTargetEl = targetEl;
      }

      let selector: string | undefined;
      let elementText: string | undefined;
      if (targetEl) {
        selector = getElementSelector(targetEl);
        elementText = (targetEl.innerText || targetEl.textContent || '').trim().slice(0, 100);
      }

      // Execute atomic action
      try {
        await this.executeSingleAction(action, targetEl, lastTargetEl);
        executedTraces.push({
          action,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          success: true,
          selector,
          elementText,
        });
      } catch (err) {
        executedTraces.push({
          action,
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          success: false,
          error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
          selector,
          elementText,
        });

        return {
          totalActions: actions.length,
          executedActions: i + 1,
          abortedEarly: true,
          abortReason: 'action_failed',
          executedTraces,
        };
      }

      // Spec: 120ms debounce between batched steps
      if (i < actions.length - 1 && this.debounceMs > 0) {
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, this.debounceMs));
      }
    }

    return {
      totalActions: actions.length,
      executedActions: actions.length,
      abortedEarly: false,
      executedTraces,
    };
  }

  private async executeSingleAction(
    action: MacroAction,
    targetEl: HTMLElement | null,
    lastTargetEl?: HTMLElement | null
  ): Promise<void> {
    const coords = action.id !== undefined ? this.registry.getCenterCoordinates(action.id) : null;

    switch (action.type) {
      case 'click': {
        if (targetEl) {
          highlightTarget(targetEl);
          if (targetEl.tagName === 'VIDEO') {
            try {
              const video = targetEl as HTMLVideoElement;
              if (video.paused) {
                video.play().catch(() => {});
              } else {
                video.pause();
              }
            } catch {}
          }
        }
        if (this.actuator) {
          const res = await this.actuator.click(coords, targetEl);
          if (!res.success) throw new Error(res.error ?? 'Click failed');
        } else if (targetEl) {
          dispatchRealisticClick(targetEl);
        } else {
          throw new Error(`Target element for click [${action.id}] not found`);
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'fill': {
        const val = action.value ?? '';
        if (targetEl) {
          highlightTarget(targetEl);
          try {
            targetEl.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center', inline: 'center', behavior: 'instant' });
          } catch {}
        }
        if (this.actuator) {
          const res = await this.actuator.type(val, coords, targetEl);
          if (!res.success) throw new Error(res.error ?? 'Fill failed');
        } else if (targetEl) {
          targetEl.focus();
          if (targetEl instanceof HTMLInputElement || targetEl instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) {
            const proto =
              targetEl instanceof HTMLInputElement
                ? window.HTMLInputElement.prototype
                : window.HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype;
            const nativeSetter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto, 'value')?.set;
            if (nativeSetter) {
              nativeSetter.call(targetEl, val);
            } else {
              targetEl.value = val;
            }
            targetEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
            targetEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
          } else if (targetEl.isContentEditable) {
            targetEl.textContent = val;
            targetEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          }
        } else {
          throw new Error(`Target element for fill [${action.id}] not found`);
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'select': {
        if (targetEl) {
          highlightTarget(targetEl);
        }
        if (targetEl instanceof HTMLSelectElement) {
          targetEl.value = action.value ?? '';
          targetEl.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
        } else if (targetEl) {
          dispatchRealisticClick(targetEl);
        } else {
          throw new Error(`Target select element [${action.id}] not found`);
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'press': {
        const key = action.value ?? 'Enter';
        const active = targetEl || lastTargetEl || (typeof document !== 'undefined' ? (document.activeElement as HTMLElement) || document.body : null);
        if (this.actuator) {
          const res = await this.actuator.pressKey(key, active);
          if (!res.success) throw new Error(res.error ?? 'Press failed');
        } else if (active) {
          const keyCode = key === 'Enter' ? 13 : key === 'Tab' ? 9 : key === 'Escape' ? 27 : key === 'Backspace' ? 8 : 0;
          const eventInit: KeyboardEventInit = {
            key,
            code: key,
            bubbles: true,
            cancelable: true,
            composed: true,
          };
          const keydown = new KeyboardEvent('keydown', eventInit);
          Object.defineProperty(keydown, 'keyCode', { get: () => keyCode });
          Object.defineProperty(keydown, 'which', { get: () => keyCode });
          active.dispatchEvent(keydown);

          const keypress = new KeyboardEvent('keypress', eventInit);
          Object.defineProperty(keypress, 'keyCode', { get: () => keyCode });
          Object.defineProperty(keypress, 'which', { get: () => keyCode });
          active.dispatchEvent(keypress);

          const keyup = new KeyboardEvent('keyup', eventInit);
          Object.defineProperty(keyup, 'keyCode', { get: () => keyCode });
          Object.defineProperty(keyup, 'which', { get: () => keyCode });
          active.dispatchEvent(keyup);

          if (key === 'Enter') {
            const form = active instanceof HTMLInputElement ? active.form : (active.closest ? active.closest('form') : null);
            if (form) {
              try {
                if (typeof form.requestSubmit === 'function') {
                  form.requestSubmit();
                } else {
                  form.submit();
                }
              } catch {}
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'scroll': {
        const dir = action.value === 'up' ? 'up' : 'down';
        if (this.actuator) {
          await this.actuator.scroll(dir);
        } else {
          smartScroll(dir, targetEl);
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'complete_media': {
        if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
          const allEls = collectAllElementsDeep(document);
          const mediaEls = allEls.filter(
            (el): el is HTMLVideoElement | HTMLAudioElement =>
              el.tagName === 'VIDEO' || el.tagName === 'AUDIO'
          );

          for (const media of mediaEls) {
            try {
              if (typeof media.play === 'function') {
                media.play().catch(() => {});
              }
              if (isFinite(media.duration) && media.duration > 0) {
                media.currentTime = Math.max(0, media.duration - 0.5);
              }
              media.playbackRate = 2.0;
              media.dispatchEvent(new Event('timeupdate', { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
              media.dispatchEvent(new Event('ended', { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
            } catch {}
          }
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'wait': {
        const ms = action.value ? parseInt(action.value, 10) : 500;
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, isNaN(ms) ? 500 : ms));
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }
  }
}

================
File: src/lib/llm/adapters/gemini.ts
================
import {
  LLMProvider,
  LLMProviderType,
  ChatMessage,
  LLMRequestOptions,
  AgentPlanResponse,
  UsageLimitError,
} from '../types';
import { parseAgentPlanResponse } from '../parser';

interface GeminiCandidate {
  content?: {
    parts?: Array<{
      text?: string;
    }>;
  };
}

interface GeminiResponse {
  candidates?: GeminiCandidate[];
  usageMetadata?: {
    promptTokenCount?: number;
    candidatesTokenCount?: number;
    totalTokenCount?: number;
    cachedContentTokenCount?: number;
  };
  error?: {
    message?: string;
  };
}

export class GeminiAdapter implements LLMProvider {
  readonly providerType: LLMProviderType = 'gemini';
  private apiKey: string;
  private defaultModel: string;

  constructor(apiKey: string, defaultModel = 'gemini-3.8-flash') {
    this.apiKey = apiKey;
    this.defaultModel = defaultModel;
  }

  async chat(messages: ChatMessage[], options?: LLMRequestOptions): Promise<AgentPlanResponse> {
    const rawText = await this.rawChat(messages, options);
    const parsed = parseAgentPlanResponse(rawText);
    return parsed;
  }

  async rawChat(messages: ChatMessage[], options?: LLMRequestOptions): Promise<string> {
    if (!this.apiKey) {
      throw new Error('Gemini API key is not configured.');
    }

    const model = options?.model ?? this.defaultModel;
    const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(
      model
    )}:generateContent?key=${encodeURIComponent(this.apiKey)}`;

    const systemMessages = messages.filter((m) => m.role === 'system');
    const nonSystemMessages = messages.filter((m) => m.role !== 'system');

    const systemInstruction =
      systemMessages.length > 0
        ? {
            parts: systemMessages.map((m) => ({
              text: typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content),
            })),
          }
        : undefined;

    const contents = nonSystemMessages.map((m) => {
      const role = m.role === 'assistant' ? 'model' : 'user';
      if (typeof m.content === 'string') {
        return {
          role,
          parts: [{ text: m.content }],
        };
      }
      return {
        role,
        parts: m.content.map((block) => {
          if (block.type === 'text') {
            return { text: block.text ?? '' };
          }
          if (block.type === 'image' && block.source) {
            return {
              inlineData: {
                mimeType: block.source.media_type,
                data: block.source.data,
              },
            };
          }
          return { text: '' };
        }),
      };
    });

    const response = await fetch(url, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'content-type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        ...(systemInstruction ? { systemInstruction } : {}),
        contents,
        generationConfig: {
          temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.1,
          maxOutputTokens: options?.maxTokens ?? 1024,
          responseMimeType: 'application/json',
        },
        safetySettings: [
          { category: 'HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT', threshold: 'BLOCK_ONLY_HIGH' },
          { category: 'HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH', threshold: 'BLOCK_ONLY_HIGH' },
          { category: 'HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT', threshold: 'BLOCK_ONLY_HIGH' },
          { category: 'HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT', threshold: 'BLOCK_ONLY_HIGH' },
        ],
      }),
      signal: options?.signal,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errText = await response.text();
      const isLimitOrUnavailable =
        response.status === 429 ||
        response.status === 404 ||
        response.status === 502 ||
        response.status === 503 ||
        /resource_exhausted|quota|rate limit|rate_limit|not found|deprecated|unavailable/i.test(errText);
      if (isLimitOrUnavailable) {
        throw new UsageLimitError(
          `Gemini model unavailable or quota exceeded (${response.status}): ${errText}`,
          'gemini',
          model,
          response.status
        );
      }
      throw new Error(`Gemini API error (${response.status}): ${errText}`);
    }

    const data = (await response.json()) as GeminiResponse & {
      promptFeedback?: { blockReason?: string };
      candidates?: Array<GeminiCandidate & { finishReason?: string }>;
    };

    if (data.error) {
      const msg = data.error.message ?? 'Unknown';
      if (/resource_exhausted|quota|rate limit|rate_limit|not found|deprecated|unavailable/i.test(msg)) {
        throw new UsageLimitError(`Gemini model error: ${msg}`, 'gemini', model);
      }
      throw new Error(`Gemini error: ${msg}`);
    }

    const candidate = data.candidates?.[0];
    if (candidate?.finishReason === 'SAFETY' || data.promptFeedback?.blockReason === 'SAFETY') {
      const reason = data.promptFeedback?.blockReason || candidate?.finishReason || 'SAFETY';
      return JSON.stringify({
        thought: `Gemini safety filter flagged this request (${reason}). Please rephrase your goal or navigate to another page.`,
        actions: [],
        complete: true,
      });
    }

    const text = candidate?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text ?? '';
    return text;
  }
}

================
File: src/lib/llm/parser.ts

import { AgentPlanResponse, MacroAction, ActionType } from './types';

const VALID_ACTION_TYPES: ReadonlySet<ActionType> = new Set([
  'click',
  'fill',
  'select',
  'press',
  'scroll',
  'wait',
  'request_vision_crop',
  'navigate',
  'go_back',
  'complete_media',
  'open_tab',
  'switch_tab',
]);

/**
 * Cleans and repairs common LLM JSON formatting flaws:
 * - Trailing commas before } or ]
 * - Smart quotes / curly quotes
 * - JavaScript comments
 */
function cleanJsonString(str: string): string {
  return str
    // Normalize unicode smart quotes to ASCII
    .replace(/[\u201C\u201D]/g, '"')
    .replace(/[\u2018\u2019]/g, "'")
    // Remove single-line comments // ... without breaking URLs (e.g. http://, https://)
    .replace(/(?<!:)\/\/[^\n\r]*/g, '')
    // Remove trailing commas before } or ]
    .replace(/,\s*([}\]])/g, '$1')
    .trim();
}

export function parseAgentPlanResponse(rawText: string): AgentPlanResponse {
  const trimmedRaw = (rawText ?? '').trim();
  if (!trimmedRaw) {
    return {
      thought: 'Empty response received from model.',
      actions: [],
      complete: false,
      rawResponse: rawText,
    };
  }

  // 1. Try markdown fenced code block extraction anywhere in the text
  let jsonCandidate = '';
  const codeBlockMatch = trimmedRaw.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*```/i);
  if (codeBlockMatch && codeBlockMatch[1]) {
    jsonCandidate = codeBlockMatch[1].trim();
  } else {
    jsonCandidate = trimmedRaw;
  }

  // 2. Find first { and last } to isolate the JSON object from surrounding commentary
  const startIdx = jsonCandidate.indexOf('{');
  const endIdx = jsonCandidate.lastIndexOf('}');

  // 3. Fallback: If no JSON braces exist, the model responded in plain text (e.g. safety notice, policy, or direct answer)
  if (startIdx === -1 || endIdx === -1 || endIdx <= startIdx) {
    return {
      thought: trimmedRaw,
      actions: [],
      complete: true, // Treat direct conversational text or safety notice as completed outcome
      rawResponse: rawText,
    };
  }

  // Isolate substring
  const isolated = jsonCandidate.substring(startIdx, endIdx + 1);

  try {
    let parsed: unknown;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(isolated);
    } catch {
      const cleaned = cleanJsonString(isolated);
      try {
        parsed = JSON.parse(cleaned);
      } catch {
        // Secondary attempt: try repairing unclosed braces if truncated
        const repaired = cleaned.endsWith('}') ? cleaned : cleaned + '}';
        parsed = JSON.parse(cleanJsonString(repaired));
      }
    }

    if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null) {
      throw new Error('Response is not an object');
    }

    const obj = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
    const thought = typeof obj.thought === 'string' ? obj.thought : '';
    let complete = false;

    if (typeof obj.complete === 'boolean') {
      complete = obj.complete;
    } else if (typeof obj.complete === 'string') {
      complete = obj.complete.trim().toLowerCase() === 'true';
    } else if (typeof (obj as any).done === 'boolean') {
      complete = (obj as any).done;
    } else if (typeof (obj as any).done === 'string') {
      complete = (obj as any).done.trim().toLowerCase() === 'true';
    } else if (typeof (obj as any).finished === 'boolean') {
      complete = (obj as any).finished;
    } else if (typeof (obj as any).status === 'string') {
      const s = (obj as any).status.toLowerCase();
      complete = s === 'completed' || s === 'complete' || s === 'done';
    }

    const actionsRaw = Array.isArray(obj.actions) ? obj.actions : [];
    const actions: MacroAction[] = [];

    for (const item of actionsRaw) {
      if (typeof item !== 'object' || item === null) continue;
      const rec = item as Record<string, unknown>;
      const rawType = String(rec.type ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!VALID_ACTION_TYPES.has(rawType as ActionType)) continue;

      const act: MacroAction = {
        type: rawType as ActionType,
      };

      if (typeof rec.id === 'number') {
        act.id = rec.id;
      } else if (typeof rec.id === 'string' && !isNaN(Number(rec.id))) {
        act.id = Number(rec.id);
      }

      if (typeof rec.value === 'string') {
        act.value = rec.value;
      }

      if (typeof rec.stop_if_url_changes === 'boolean') {
        act.stop_if_url_changes = rec.stop_if_url_changes;
      }

      actions.push(act);
    }

    // Heuristic: If model provided 0 actions and thought explicitly states completion, treat as complete
    if (!complete && actions.length === 0 && thought.length > 0) {
      const lowerThought = thought.toLowerCase();
      const completionKeywords = [
        'task complete',
        'task is complete',
        'goal achieved',
        'goal is achieved',
        'goal completed',
        'completed the task',
        'finished the task',
        'found the answer',
        'here is the answer',
        'successfully finished',
        'no further action',
        'all steps completed',
      ];
      if (completionKeywords.some((kw) => lowerThought.includes(kw))) {
        complete = true;
      }
    }

    return {
      thought,
      actions,
      complete,
      rawResponse: rawText,
    };
  } catch {
    // Graceful recovery: If JSON parsing failed despite containing braces,
    // extract any thought string via regex or return trimmed text directly rather than crashing
    const thoughtMatch = rawText.match(/"thought"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"/i);
    const recoveredThought = thoughtMatch ? thoughtMatch[1] : trimmedRaw;

    return {
      thought: recoveredThought,
      actions: [],
      complete: true,
      rawResponse: rawText,
    };
  }
}

File: tests/actuator-and-vision.test.ts
