import { ChatMessage, AgentPlanResponse } from './types';

export interface TokenEstimationMetrics {
  totalEstimatedTokens: number;
  cachedPrefixTokens: number;
  freshTokens: number;
  estimatedCostDiscountPercent: number;
}

export const AGENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are Vision AI, an ultra-low-token autonomous Chrome browser agent.
Your objective is to accomplish the user's browser goal efficiently and accurately through real browser interactions.

CORE OPERATIONAL MANDATES:
1. ACTION-FIRST PRINCIPLE:
   - You are an autonomous browser agent executing on behalf of the user.
   - For broad or ambitious goals (e.g., "complete this course", "buy tickets", "apply for job", "research AI"):
     DO NOT decline, lecture, refuse, give up, or output disclaimers.
     ALWAYS take the immediate next concrete step forward on the active page (e.g. click Module 1, open Lesson 1, click Start, expand chapters, advance slides, or fill inputs).
   - Never return an empty actions array [] on early steps unless the user explicitly asked a pure informational question (e.g. "what is the price?") and the answer is already visible in the viewport DOM.

2. AVAILABLE MACRO ACTIONS:
- click: { type: "click", id: number, stop_if_url_changes?: boolean }
- fill: { type: "fill", id: number, value: string }
- select: { type: "select", id: number, value: string }
- press: { type: "press", id?: number, value: "Enter"|"Tab"|"Escape"|"Backspace" }
- scroll: { type: "scroll", value: "up"|"down" }
- wait: { type: "wait", value: "500" }
- request_vision_crop: { type: "request_vision_crop", id: number }
- navigate: { type: "navigate", value: "https://..." }
- go_back: { type: "go_back" }
- complete_media: { type: "complete_media" }
- open_tab: { type: "open_tab", value: "https://..." }
- switch_tab: { type: "switch_tab", value: "<tabId or url substring>" }

3. GUIDELINES:
- Macro-Batching: You can batch sequential actions together in one turn (e.g. click an accordion [id] or fill an input [id] and press Enter).
- Completing the Goal:
  - Only set "complete": true when the requested task is genuinely finished or the question is fully answered.
  - Provide your final answer, summary, or confirmation in "thought".
- Anti-Stall: If an action produces no change, do not repeat it. Try an alternate element, scroll, or proceed to the next section.
- Output Format Invariant:
  - Output ONLY the JSON object. Do not output conversational preambles, disclaimers, or text outside the JSON.

RESPONSE FORMAT:
{
  "thought": "Brief action rationale describing what you are clicking or doing next, or final answer when complete",
  "actions": [
    { "type": "click", "id": 1 }
  ],
  "complete": false
}`;

/**
 * Fast deterministic string hash for prefix stability verification.
 */
function fastHash(str: string): string {
  let hash = 5381;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    hash = (hash * 33) ^ str.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  return (hash >>> 0).toString(16);
}

/**
 * Estimates token count for text.
 * Viewport DOM text contains tags and brackets, yielding ~3.8 characters per token.
 */
export function estimateTokenCount(text: string): number {
  if (!text) return 0;
  // Viewport DOM / code tokens: ~3.8 chars per token, minimum 1 token
  return Math.ceil(text.length / 3.8);
}

export function estimateMessagesTokens(messages: ChatMessage[]): number {
  let total = 0;
  for (const msg of messages) {
    if (typeof msg.content === 'string') {
      total += estimateTokenCount(msg.content) + 4; // 4 token message framing overhead
    } else {
      for (const block of msg.content) {
        if (block.type === 'text' && block.text) {
          total += estimateTokenCount(block.text);
        } else if (block.type === 'image') {
          total += 258; // Standard Claude/Gemini low-res image token cost
        }
      }
      total += 4;
    }
  }
  return total;
}

export class ByteStableContextManager {
  private messages: ChatMessage[] = [];
  private initialPrefixHash = '';
  private prefixMessageCount = 0;
  private prefixTokenCount = 0;

  constructor() {
    this.messages = [];
  }

  /**
   * Initializes the run with System Prompt, User Goal, and Step 1 Distilled DOM.
   * This initial prefix is frozen and must remain 100% byte-identical across all turns.
   */
  initializeRun(goal: string, step1DistilledDom: string): void {
    this.messages = [
      {
        role: 'system',
        content: AGENT_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
        cache_control: { type: 'ephemeral' },
      },
      {
        role: 'user',
        content: `USER GOAL: ${goal}\n\nINITIAL VIEWPORT DOM (Step 1):\n${step1DistilledDom}`,
        cache_control: { type: 'ephemeral' },
      },
    ];

    this.prefixMessageCount = this.messages.length;
    this.initialPrefixHash = this.computePrefixHash();
    this.prefixTokenCount = estimateMessagesTokens(this.messages);
  }

  private computePrefixHash(): string {
    const prefixTexts = this.messages.slice(0, this.prefixMessageCount).map((m) => {
      return `${m.role}:${typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content)}`;
    });
    return fastHash(prefixTexts.join('||'));
  }

  /**
   * Verifies that the initial prompt prefix has not mutated.
   */
  verifyPrefixStability(): boolean {
    if (this.messages.length < this.prefixMessageCount) {
      return false;
    }
    const currentPrefixHash = this.computePrefixHash();
    return currentPrefixHash === this.initialPrefixHash;
  }

  /**
   * Strictly appends an assistant plan to the end of history without altering prefix.
   */
  appendAssistantPlan(plan: AgentPlanResponse): void {
    if (!this.verifyPrefixStability()) {
      throw new Error('Prefix stability invariant violated before appending plan!');
    }

    const jsonContent = JSON.stringify(
      {
        thought: plan.thought,
        actions: plan.actions,
        complete: plan.complete,
      },
      null,
      2
    );

    this.messages.push({
      role: 'assistant',
      content: jsonContent,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Strictly appends a user step delta/observation without altering prefix.
   */
  appendStepObservation(stepNumber: number, observationOrDelta: string): void {
    if (!this.verifyPrefixStability()) {
      throw new Error('Prefix stability invariant violated before appending observation!');
    }

    this.messages.push({
      role: 'user',
      content: `[STEP ${stepNumber} OBSERVATION]\n${observationOrDelta}`,
    });
  }

  getMessages(): ChatMessage[] {
    if (!this.verifyPrefixStability()) {
      throw new Error('Byte-stable prefix invariant violated!');
    }
    // Return shallow copy of array to prevent external push/pop mutations
    return [...this.messages];
  }

  getMessageCount(): number {
    return this.messages.length;
  }

  getPrefixTokenCount(): number {
    return this.prefixTokenCount;
  }

  /**
   * Calculates token usage and estimated cache savings.
   */
  getTelemetryMetrics(): TokenEstimationMetrics {
    const totalEstimatedTokens = estimateMessagesTokens(this.messages);
    const cachedPrefixTokens = this.prefixTokenCount;
    const freshTokens = Math.max(0, totalEstimatedTokens - cachedPrefixTokens);

    // Cached prefix tokens typically receive 90% discount on Claude / Gemini
    const billableEffective = freshTokens + cachedPrefixTokens * 0.1;
    const estimatedCostDiscountPercent =
      totalEstimatedTokens > 0
        ? Math.round(((totalEstimatedTokens - billableEffective) / totalEstimatedTokens) * 100)
        : 0;

    return {
      totalEstimatedTokens,
      cachedPrefixTokens,
      freshTokens,
      estimatedCostDiscountPercent,
    };
  }
}

File: src/lib/llm/types.ts

export type LLMProviderType = 'anthropic' | 'gemini' | 'groq' | 'openrouter' | 'ollama';

export type RouterTier = 0 | 1 | 2;

export type ActionType =
  | 'click'
  | 'fill'
  | 'select'
  | 'press'
  | 'scroll'
  | 'wait'
  | 'request_vision_crop'
  | 'navigate'
  | 'go_back'
  | 'complete_media'
  | 'open_tab'
  | 'switch_tab';

export interface MacroAction {
  type: ActionType;
  id?: number;
  value?: string;
  stop_if_url_changes?: boolean;
}

export interface AgentPlanResponse {
  thought: string;
  actions: MacroAction[];
  complete: boolean;
  rawResponse?: string;
  usage?: TokenUsage;
}

export interface TokenUsage {
  promptTokens: number;
  completionTokens: number;
  cachedTokens: number;
  totalTokens: number;
}

export interface MessageContentBlock {
  type: 'text' | 'image';
  text?: string;
  source?: {
    type: 'base64';
    media_type: string;
    data: string;
  };
  cache_control?: {
    type: 'ephemeral';
  };
}

export interface ChatMessage {
  role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string | MessageContentBlock[];
  cache_control?: {
    type: 'ephemeral';
  };
}

export interface LLMRequestOptions {
  model?: string;
  temperature?: number;
  maxTokens?: number;
  responseFormat?: 'json' | 'text';
  signal?: AbortSignal;
}

export interface ProviderApiKeys {
  anthropic?: string;
  gemini?: string;
  groq?: string;
  openrouter?: string;
  ollamaBaseUrl?: string;
}

export interface RouterConfig {
  apiKeys: ProviderApiKeys;
  tier0Model?: string; // e.g. "llama3.2:latest"
  tier1Model?: string; // e.g. "llama-3.3-70b-versatile" or "gemini-2.5-flash"
  tier2Model?: string; // e.g. "claude-3-7-sonnet-20250219" or "gemini-2.5-pro"
  preferredTier1Provider?: 'groq' | 'gemini' | 'openrouter';
  preferredTier2Provider?: 'anthropic' | 'gemini' | 'openrouter';
  openRouterModel?: string;
  openRouterFallbackModels?: string[];
  groqModel?: string;
  groqFallbackModels?: string[];
  geminiModel?: string;
  geminiFallbackModels?: string[];
  anthropicModel?: string;
  autoFallbackOnLimit?: boolean;
}

export class UsageLimitError extends Error {
  readonly provider: LLMProviderType;
  readonly model?: string;
  readonly statusCode?: number;

  constructor(message: string, provider: LLMProviderType, model?: string, statusCode?: number) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'UsageLimitError';
    this.provider = provider;
    this.model = model;
    this.statusCode = statusCode;
  }
}

export interface LLMProvider {
  readonly providerType: LLMProviderType;
  chat(messages: ChatMessage[], options?: LLMRequestOptions): Promise<AgentPlanResponse>;
  rawChat?(messages: ChatMessage[], options?: LLMRequestOptions): Promise<string>;
}

import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import {
  LLMRouter,
  ByteStableContextManager,
  AnthropicAdapter,
  GroqAdapter,
  GeminiAdapter,
  OllamaAdapter,
  ChatMessage,
  AgentPlanResponse,
} from '../src/lib/llm';

describe('M1.3: Multi-Provider Router & Byte-Stable Context Manager Suite', () => {
  const originalFetch = globalThis.fetch;

  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    globalThis.fetch = originalFetch;
  });

  describe('Tiered Routing Selection & Auto-Escalation', () => {
    const testConfig = {
      apiKeys: {
        anthropic: 'sk-ant-test-key-123',
        gemini: 'test-gemini-key-456',
        groq: 'gsk-test-groq-key-789',
        openrouter: 'sk-or-test-key-abc',
        ollamaBaseUrl: 'http://localhost:11434',
      },
    };

    it('routes Tier 0 to Ollama adapter for local zero-token processing', () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter(testConfig);
      const { provider, activeTier } = router.getProviderForTier(0);
      expect(activeTier).toBe(0);
      expect(provider.providerType).toBe('ollama');
      expect(provider).toBeInstanceOf(OllamaAdapter);
    });

    it('routes Tier 1 to Groq adapter by default for ultra-low latency execution', () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter(testConfig);
      const { provider, activeTier } = router.getProviderForTier(1);
      expect(activeTier).toBe(1);
      expect(provider.providerType).toBe('groq');
      expect(provider).toBeInstanceOf(GroqAdapter);
    });

    it('routes Tier 2 to Anthropic adapter for complex reasoning and decomposition', () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter(testConfig);
      const { provider, activeTier } = router.getProviderForTier(2);
      expect(activeTier).toBe(2);
      expect(provider.providerType).toBe('anthropic');
      expect(provider).toBeInstanceOf(AnthropicAdapter);
    });

    it('auto-escalates Tier 1 to Tier 2 after 2 consecutive action failures', () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter(testConfig);
      expect(router.getConsecutiveFailures()).toBe(0);

      // 1st failure: still Tier 1
      router.recordFailure();
      expect(router.getConsecutiveFailures()).toBe(1);
      let resolved = router.getProviderForTier(1);
      expect(resolved.activeTier).toBe(1);
      expect(resolved.provider.providerType).toBe('groq');

      // 2nd failure: escalates to Tier 2
      router.recordFailure();
      expect(router.getConsecutiveFailures()).toBe(2);
      resolved = router.getProviderForTier(1);
      expect(resolved.activeTier).toBe(2);
      expect(resolved.provider.providerType).toBe('anthropic');

      // Reset restores normal Tier 1 routing
      router.resetFailures();
      expect(router.getConsecutiveFailures()).toBe(0);
      resolved = router.getProviderForTier(1);
      expect(resolved.activeTier).toBe(1);
      expect(resolved.provider.providerType).toBe('groq');
    });

    it('respects user preference overrides for Tier 1 and Tier 2', () => {
      const customRouter = new LLMRouter({
        ...testConfig,
        preferredTier1Provider: 'gemini',
        preferredTier2Provider: 'gemini',
      });

      const tier1 = customRouter.getProviderForTier(1);
      expect(tier1.activeTier).toBe(1);
      expect(tier1.provider).toBeInstanceOf(GeminiAdapter);

      const tier2 = customRouter.getProviderForTier(2);
      expect(tier2.activeTier).toBe(2);
      expect(tier2.provider).toBeInstanceOf(GeminiAdapter);
    });
  });

  describe('Anthropic cache_control and Browser Access Header Placement', () => {
    it('properly injects anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access and cache_control', async () => {
      let capturedHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
      let capturedBody: any = null;

      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
        capturedHeaders = (init.headers ?? {}) as Record<string, string>;
        capturedBody = JSON.parse(init.body as string);

        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            content: [
              {
                type: 'text',
                text: JSON.stringify({
                  thought: 'Clicking search button',
                  actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 4 }],
                  complete: false,
                }),
              },
            ],
            usage: {
              input_tokens: 450,
              output_tokens: 35,
              cache_read_input_tokens: 380,
            },
          }),
        };
      });

      const adapter = new AnthropicAdapter('sk-ant-test-api-key');
      const messages: ChatMessage[] = [
        {
          role: 'system',
          content: 'System prompt instructions',
          cache_control: { type: 'ephemeral' },
        },
        {
          role: 'user',
          content: 'Step 1 full distilled DOM tree',
          cache_control: { type: 'ephemeral' },
        },
      ];

      const res = await adapter.chat(messages);
      expect(res.thought).toBe('Clicking search button');
      expect(res.actions).toEqual([{ type: 'click', id: 4 }]);
      expect(res.complete).toBe(false);

      // Verify browser direct access header
      expect(capturedHeaders['anthropic-dangerous-direct-browser-access']).toBe('true');
      expect(capturedHeaders['x-api-key']).toBe('sk-ant-test-api-key');

      // Verify system cache_control placement
      expect(capturedBody.system).toBeDefined();
      expect(capturedBody.system[0].cache_control).toEqual({ type: 'ephemeral' });

      // Verify user message cache_control placement
      expect(capturedBody.messages[0].content[0].cache_control).toEqual({ type: 'ephemeral' });
    });
  });

  describe('Byte-Stable Prefix Invariance Across 5 Simulated Steps', () => {
    it('maintains 100% byte-identical prefix across 5 turns with append-only growth', () => {
      const manager = new ByteStableContextManager();

      const userGoal = 'Search for latest AI papers on arXiv and download the first PDF';
      const step1Dom = `[1] <input type="text" placeholder="Search arXiv" value="">\n[2] <button type="submit">Search</button>`;

      // Initialize Step 1
      manager.initializeRun(userGoal, step1Dom);
      expect(manager.verifyPrefixStability()).toBe(true);

      const initialMessages = manager.getMessages();
      const initialSystemMsg = initialMessages[0];
      const initialUserMsg = initialMessages[1];
      const initialPrefixTokenCount = manager.getPrefixTokenCount();

      expect(initialPrefixTokenCount).toBeGreaterThan(0);

      // Simulate 5 consecutive turns
      for (let step = 1; step <= 5; step++) {
        const plan: AgentPlanResponse = {
          thought: `Executing step ${step} action sequence`,
          actions: [{ type: 'click', id: step }],
          complete: step === 5,
        };

        // Append assistant plan
        manager.appendAssistantPlan(plan);
        expect(manager.verifyPrefixStability()).toBe(true);

        if (step < 5) {
          // Append incremental step delta
          const delta = `[DELTA]\nADDED:\n[${step + 10}] <div role="alert">Step ${step} completed</div>`;
          manager.appendStepObservation(step + 1, delta);
          expect(manager.verifyPrefixStability()).toBe(true);
        }

        // Verify the first two messages (System Prompt & Initial Distilled DOM) remain byte-identical
        const currentMessages = manager.getMessages();
        expect(currentMessages[0]).toEqual(initialSystemMsg);
        expect(currentMessages[1]).toEqual(initialUserMsg);
      }

      // Check telemetry metrics
      const metrics = manager.getTelemetryMetrics();
      expect(metrics.cachedPrefixTokens).toBe(initialPrefixTokenCount);
      expect(metrics.totalEstimatedTokens).toBeGreaterThan(metrics.cachedPrefixTokens);
      expect(metrics.freshTokens).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(metrics.estimatedCostDiscountPercent).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(40);

      // Verify total turns: 1 system + 1 user initial + 5 assistant plans + 4 delta observations = 11 messages
      expect(manager.getMessageCount()).toBe(11);
    });

    it('rejects mutation if prefix is tampered with', () => {
      const manager = new ByteStableContextManager();
      manager.initializeRun('Goal', 'Initial DOM');

      // Tamper with private array via any cast in test
      const messagesInternal = (manager as any).messages;
      messagesInternal[1].content = 'Tampered content';

      expect(manager.verifyPrefixStability()).toBe(false);
      expect(() => {
        manager.appendAssistantPlan({ thought: '', actions: [], complete: false });
      }).toThrow(/Prefix stability invariant violated/);
    });
  });

  describe('Model Selection & Automatic Failover on Usage Limits', () => {
    it('respects specific model preferences for providers', () => {
      const router = new LLMRouter({
        apiKeys: {
          openrouter: 'sk-or-test-key',
          groq: 'gsk-test-key',
          gemini: 'aiza-test-key',
        },
        preferredTier1Provider: 'openrouter',
        openRouterModel: 'deepseek/deepseek-chat:free',
        groqModel: 'llama-3.1-8b-instant',
        geminiModel: 'gemini-2.0-flash',
      });

      const tier1 = router.getProviderForTier(1);
      expect(tier1.provider.providerType).toBe('openrouter');
      expect(tier1.model).toBe('deepseek/deepseek-chat:free');

      router.updateConfig({ preferredTier1Provider: 'groq' });
      const tier1Groq = router.getProviderForTier(1);
      expect(tier1Groq.provider.providerType).toBe('groq');
      expect(tier1Groq.model).toBe('llama-3.1-8b-instant');
    });

    it('automatically switches OpenRouter model on HTTP 429 rate limit without interrupting task', async () => {
      let callCount = 0;
      const requestedModels: string[] = [];

      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (_url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
        callCount++;
        const body = JSON.parse(init.body as string);
        requestedModels.push(body.model);

        if (callCount === 1) {
          // First attempt hits 429 Rate Limit
          return {
            ok: false,
            status: 429,
            text: async () => 'Rate limit exceeded: Please wait or try another model.',
          };
        }

        // Second attempt with fallback model succeeds
        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            choices: [
              {
                message: {
                  content: JSON.stringify({
                    thought: 'Auto-switched model executed successfully',
                    actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 5 }],
                    complete: true,
                  }),
                },
              },
            ],
          }),
        };
      });

      const router = new LLMRouter({
        apiKeys: {
          openrouter: 'sk-or-key-123',
        },
        preferredTier1Provider: 'openrouter',
        openRouterModel: 'meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct:free',
        openRouterFallbackModels: [
          'meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct:free',
          'google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free',
        ],
        autoFallbackOnLimit: true,
      });

      const messages: ChatMessage[] = [
        { role: 'user', content: 'Navigate to research portal' },
      ];

      const result = await router.executeStep(messages, 1);

      expect(callCount).toBe(2);
      expect(requestedModels[0]).toBe('meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct:free');
      expect(requestedModels[1]).toBe('google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free');
      expect(result.response.thought).toBe('Auto-switched model executed successfully');
      expect(result.response.complete).toBe(true);
      expect(result.modelUsed).toBe('google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free');
      expect(result.fallbackNotice).toContain('google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free');
      // Subsequent step should use the working fallback model
      expect(router.getConfig().openRouterModel).toBe('google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free');
    });

    it('automatically catches HTTP 404 model unavailable for free error and fails over to openrouter/free', async () => {
      let callCount = 0;
      const requestedModels: string[] = [];

      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (_url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
        callCount++;
        const body = JSON.parse(init.body as string);
        requestedModels.push(body.model);

        if (callCount === 1) {
          // Exact error string reported by user
          return {
            ok: false,
            status: 404,
            text: async () =>
              JSON.stringify({
                error: {
                  message:
                    'This model is unavailable for free. The paid version is available now - use this slug instead: meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct',
                  code: 404,
                },
              }),
          };
        }

        // Second call with openrouter/free succeeds
        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            choices: [
              {
                message: {
                  content: JSON.stringify({
                    thought: 'openrouter/free selected working model and completed task',
                    actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 2 }],
                    complete: true,
                  }),
                },
              },
            ],
          }),
        };
      });

      const router = new LLMRouter({
        apiKeys: {
          openrouter: 'sk-or-key-test',
        },
        preferredTier1Provider: 'openrouter',
        openRouterModel: 'meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct:free',
        openRouterFallbackModels: ['openrouter/free', 'google/gemma-4-31b-it:free'],
        autoFallbackOnLimit: true,
      });

      const result = await router.executeStep([{ role: 'user', content: 'Search documents' }], 1);

      expect(callCount).toBe(2);
      expect(requestedModels[0]).toBe('meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct:free');
      expect(requestedModels[1]).toBe('openrouter/free');
      expect(result.response.complete).toBe(true);
      expect(result.modelUsed).toBe('openrouter/free');
      expect(result.fallbackNotice).toContain('openrouter/free');
      expect(router.getConfig().openRouterModel).toBe('openrouter/free');
    });

    it('cross-provider failover: automatically falls over from Groq to OpenRouter when 429 occurs', async () => {
      let groqCalled = false;
      let openRouterCalled = false;

      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (url: string, _init: RequestInit) => {
        if (url.includes('api.groq.com')) {
          groqCalled = true;
          return {
            ok: false,
            status: 429,
            text: async () => 'Rate limit exceeded: TPM limit reached',
          };
        }

        if (url.includes('openrouter.ai')) {
          openRouterCalled = true;
          return {
            ok: true,
            status: 200,
            json: async () => ({
              choices: [
                {
                  message: {
                    content: JSON.stringify({
                      thought: 'OpenRouter fallback executed',
                      actions: [{ type: 'wait' }],
                      complete: false,
                    }),
                  },
                },
              ],
            }),
          };
        }

        return { ok: false, status: 500, text: async () => 'Unexpected URL' };
      });

      const router = new LLMRouter({
        apiKeys: {
          groq: 'gsk-key',
          openrouter: 'sk-or-key',
        },
        preferredTier1Provider: 'groq',
        autoFallbackOnLimit: true,
      });

      const result = await router.executeStep([{ role: 'user', content: 'Test prompt' }], 1);

      expect(groqCalled).toBe(true);
      expect(openRouterCalled).toBe(true);
      expect(result.provider).toBe('openrouter');
      expect(result.response.thought).toBe('OpenRouter fallback executed');
      expect(result.fallbackNotice).toContain('openrouter');
    });

    it('automatically switches Groq model on 404 decommissioned or 429 rate limit', async () => {
      let callCount = 0;
      const modelsCalled: string[] = [];

      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (_url: string, init: RequestInit) => {
        callCount++;
        const body = JSON.parse(init.body as string);
        modelsCalled.push(body.model);

        if (callCount === 1) {
          // Model decommissioned / 404
          return {
            ok: false,
            status: 404,
            text: async () => 'model_decommissioned: The model is decommissioned',
          };
        }

        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            choices: [
              {
                message: {
                  content: JSON.stringify({
                    thought: 'Groq fallback model succeeded',
                    actions: [{ type: 'wait' }],
                    complete: true,
                  }),
                },
              },
            ],
          }),
        };
      });

      const router = new LLMRouter({
        apiKeys: { groq: 'gsk-test' },
        preferredTier1Provider: 'groq',
        groqModel: 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile',
        groqFallbackModels: ['llama-3.3-70b-versatile', 'llama-3.1-8b-instant'],
        autoFallbackOnLimit: true,
      });

      const result = await router.executeStep([{ role: 'user', content: 'test' }], 1);

      expect(callCount).toBe(2);
      expect(modelsCalled[0]).toBe('llama-3.3-70b-versatile');
      expect(modelsCalled[1]).toBe('llama-3.1-8b-instant');
      expect(result.provider).toBe('groq');
      expect(result.modelUsed).toBe('llama-3.1-8b-instant');
      expect(result.fallbackNotice).toContain('llama-3.1-8b-instant');
      expect(router.getConfig().groqModel).toBe('llama-3.1-8b-instant');
    });

    it('automatically switches Gemini model on RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED or 429 quota error', async () => {
      let callCount = 0;
      const modelsCalled: string[] = [];

      globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockImplementation(async (url: string, _init: RequestInit) => {
        callCount++;
        const modelMatch = url.match(/models\/([^:]+):generateContent/);
        if (modelMatch) modelsCalled.push(modelMatch[1]);

        if (callCount === 1) {
          return {
            ok: false,
            status: 429,
            text: async () => 'RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED: Quota exceeded for quota group GenerateContentRequestsPerMinute',
          };
        }

        return {
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          json: async () => ({
            candidates: [
              {
                content: {
                  parts: [
                    {
                      text: JSON.stringify({
                        thought: 'Gemini fallback model completed step',
                        actions: [{ type: 'click', id: 10 }],
                        complete: true,
                      }),
                    },
                  ],
                },
              },
            ],
          }),
        };
      });

      const router = new LLMRouter({
        apiKeys: { gemini: 'aiza-test' },
        preferredTier1Provider: 'gemini',
        geminiModel: 'gemini-3.8-flash',
        geminiFallbackModels: ['gemini-3.8-flash', 'gemini-3.6-flash'],
        autoFallbackOnLimit: true,
      });

      const result = await router.executeStep([{ role: 'user', content: 'test' }], 1);

      expect(callCount).toBe(2);
      expect(modelsCalled[0]).toBe('gemini-3.8-flash');
      expect(modelsCalled[1]).toBe('gemini-3.6-flash');
      expect(result.provider).toBe('gemini');
      expect(result.modelUsed).toBe('gemini-3.6-flash');
      expect(result.fallbackNotice).toContain('gemini-3.6-flash');
      expect(router.getConfig().geminiModel).toBe('gemini-3.6-flash');
    });
  });
});

================
File: src/lib/llm/router.ts
================
import {
  LLMProvider,
  LLMProviderType,
  RouterTier,
  RouterConfig,
  ChatMessage,
  LLMRequestOptions,
  AgentPlanResponse,
  UsageLimitError,
} from './types';
import { AnthropicAdapter } from './adapters/anthropic';
import { GeminiAdapter } from './adapters/gemini';
import { GroqAdapter } from './adapters/groq';
import { OpenRouterAdapter } from './adapters/openrouter';
import { OllamaAdapter } from './adapters/ollama';

export const DEFAULT_OPENROUTER_FALLBACKS = [
  'openrouter/free',
  'google/gemma-4-31b-it:free',
  'google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free',
  'nvidia/nemotron-3-super-120b-a12b:free',
  'cohere/north-mini-code:free',
  'liquid/lfm-2.5-2.6b:free',
];

export const DEFAULT_GROQ_FALLBACKS = [
  'llama-3.3-70b-versatile',
  'llama-3.1-8b-instant',
  'deepseek-r1-distill-llama-70b',
  'mixtral-8x7b-32768',
  'gemma2-9b-it',
];

export const DEFAULT_GEMINI_FALLBACKS = [
  'gemini-3.8-flash',
  'gemini-3.6-flash',
  'gemini-3.1-pro-preview',
  'gemini-4-argon',
  'gemini-3-flash-preview',
  'gemini-3.5-flash-lite',
  'gemini-2.5-flash',
  'gemini-2.5-pro',
];

export interface StepExecutionResult {
  response: AgentPlanResponse;
  activeTier: RouterTier;
  provider: LLMProviderType;
  modelUsed?: string;
  fallbackNotice?: string;
}

export class LLMRouter {
  private config: RouterConfig;
  private consecutiveFailures = 0;

  constructor(config: RouterConfig) {
    this.config = config;
  }

  updateConfig(config: Partial<RouterConfig>): void {
    this.config = {
      ...this.config,
      ...config,
      apiKeys: {
        ...this.config.apiKeys,
        ...config.apiKeys,
      },
    };
  }

  getConfig(): Readonly<RouterConfig> {
    return this.config;
  }

  recordFailure(): void {
    this.consecutiveFailures += 1;
  }

  resetFailures(): void {
    this.consecutiveFailures = 0;
  }

  getConsecutiveFailures(): number {
    return this.consecutiveFailures;
  }

  private persistModelPreference(field: keyof RouterConfig, value: string): void {
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.storage?.local) {
      chrome.storage.local.get('modelRoutingConfig').then((res) => {
        const current = (res.modelRoutingConfig as Record<string, unknown>) || {};
        chrome.storage.local
          .set({
            modelRoutingConfig: { ...current, [field]: value },
          })
          .catch(() => {});
      });
    }
  }

  /**
   * Resolves the provider for a specific tier.
   * Auto-escalates from Tier 1 to Tier 2 if consecutiveFailures >= 2.
   */
  getProviderForTier(requestedTier: RouterTier = 1): { provider: LLMProvider; activeTier: RouterTier; model?: string } {
    const keys = this.config.apiKeys;
    let tier: RouterTier = requestedTier;

    // Spec rule: Escalate to Tier 2 after 2 consecutive action failures
    if (tier === 1 && this.consecutiveFailures >= 2) {
      tier = 2;
    }

    if (tier === 0) {
      return {
        provider: new OllamaAdapter(keys.ollamaBaseUrl, this.config.tier0Model ?? 'llama3.2:latest'),
        activeTier: 0,
        model: this.config.tier0Model,
      };
    }

    if (tier === 1) {
      const pref = this.config.preferredTier1Provider ?? 'groq';

      if (pref === 'groq' && keys.groq) {
        const model = this.config.groqModel ?? this.config.tier1Model ?? 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile';
        return {
          provider: new GroqAdapter(keys.groq, model),
          activeTier: 1,
          model,
        };
      }

      if (pref === 'openrouter' && keys.openrouter) {
        const model = this.config.openRouterModel ?? this.config.tier1Model ?? 'openrouter/free';
        return {
          provider: new OpenRouterAdapter(keys.openrouter, model),
          activeTier: 1,
          model,
        };
      }

      if (pref === 'gemini' && keys.gemini) {
        const model = this.config.geminiModel ?? this.config.tier1Model ?? 'gemini-3.8-flash';
        return {
          provider: new GeminiAdapter(keys.gemini, model),
          activeTier: 1,
          model,
        };
      }

      if (keys.groq) {
        const model = this.config.groqModel ?? this.config.tier1Model ?? 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile';
        return {
          provider: new GroqAdapter(keys.groq, model),
          activeTier: 1,
          model,
        };
      }

      if (keys.openrouter) {
        const model = this.config.openRouterModel ?? this.config.tier1Model ?? 'openrouter/free';
        return {
          provider: new OpenRouterAdapter(keys.openrouter, model),
          activeTier: 1,
          model,
        };
      }

      if (keys.gemini) {
        const model = this.config.geminiModel ?? this.config.tier1Model ?? 'gemini-3.8-flash';
        return {
          provider: new GeminiAdapter(keys.gemini, model),
          activeTier: 1,
          model,
        };
      }

      // Fallback to Tier 2 if Tier 1 keys are missing
      tier = 2;
    }

    // Tier 2: Claude Sonnet or Gemini Pro or OpenRouter
    const pref2 = this.config.preferredTier2Provider ?? 'anthropic';

    if (pref2 === 'gemini' && keys.gemini) {
      const model = this.config.geminiModel ?? this.config.tier2Model ?? 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
      return {
        provider: new GeminiAdapter(keys.gemini, model),
        activeTier: 2,
        model,
      };
    }

    if (pref2 === 'openrouter' && keys.openrouter) {
      const model = this.config.openRouterModel ?? this.config.tier2Model ?? 'anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet';
      return {
        provider: new OpenRouterAdapter(keys.openrouter, model),
        activeTier: 2,
        model,
      };
    }

    if (keys.anthropic) {
      const model = this.config.anthropicModel ?? this.config.tier2Model ?? 'claude-3-7-sonnet-20250219';
      return {
        provider: new AnthropicAdapter(keys.anthropic, model),
        activeTier: 2,
        model,
      };
    }

    if (keys.gemini) {
      const model = this.config.geminiModel ?? this.config.tier2Model ?? 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
      return {
        provider: new GeminiAdapter(keys.gemini, model),
        activeTier: 2,
        model,
      };
    }

    if (keys.openrouter) {
      const model = this.config.openRouterModel ?? this.config.tier2Model ?? 'anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet';
      return {
        provider: new OpenRouterAdapter(keys.openrouter, model),
        activeTier: 2,
        model,
      };
    }

    // Local fallback if no cloud keys configured
    return {
      provider: new OllamaAdapter(keys.ollamaBaseUrl, 'llama3.2:latest'),
      activeTier: 0,
      model: 'llama3.2:latest',
    };
  }

  async executeStep(
    messages: ChatMessage[],
    requestedTier: RouterTier = 1,
    options?: LLMRequestOptions
  ): Promise<StepExecutionResult> {
    const { provider, activeTier, model: initialModel } = this.getProviderForTier(requestedTier);
    const activeModel = options?.model ?? initialModel;

    try {
      const plan = await provider.chat(messages, {
        ...options,
        model: activeModel,
      });
      return {
        response: plan,
        activeTier,
        provider: provider.providerType,
        modelUsed: activeModel,
      };
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const isLimitOrUnavailable =
        err instanceof UsageLimitError ||
        (err instanceof Error &&
          /unavailable|rate limit|rate_limit|quota|credit|insufficient|429|402|404|503|502|resource_exhausted|not found|no endpoints|paid version|free tier/i.test(
            err.message
          ));

      if (!isLimitOrUnavailable || this.config.autoFallbackOnLimit === false) {
        throw err;
      }

      console.warn(
        `[Vision AI Router] Model unavailable or limit reached on ${provider.providerType} (${activeModel}). Attempting automatic seamless model switch...`
      );

      // Strategy 1: Intra-provider model fallback
      if (provider.providerType === 'openrouter') {
        const fallbacks =
          this.config.openRouterFallbackModels && this.config.openRouterFallbackModels.length > 0
            ? this.config.openRouterFallbackModels
            : DEFAULT_OPENROUTER_FALLBACKS;

        for (const candidateModel of fallbacks) {
          if (candidateModel === activeModel) continue;
          try {
            console.log(`[Vision AI Router] Auto-switching to OpenRouter fallback model: ${candidateModel}`);
            const plan = await provider.chat(messages, {
              ...options,
              model: candidateModel,
            });
            this.config.openRouterModel = candidateModel;
            this.persistModelPreference('openRouterModel', candidateModel);
            return {
              response: plan,
              activeTier,
              provider: 'openrouter',
              modelUsed: candidateModel,
              fallbackNotice: `Auto-switched to ${candidateModel} (${activeModel} was unavailable/limited)`,
            };
          } catch (nextErr) {
            console.warn(`[Vision AI Router] Fallback candidate ${candidateModel} also failed:`, nextErr);
          }
        }
      } else if (provider.providerType === 'groq') {
        const fallbacks =
          this.config.groqFallbackModels && this.config.groqFallbackModels.length > 0
            ? this.config.groqFallbackModels
            : DEFAULT_GROQ_FALLBACKS;

        for (const candidateModel of fallbacks) {
          if (candidateModel === activeModel) continue;
          try {
            console.log(`[Vision AI Router] Auto-switching to Groq fallback model: ${candidateModel}`);
            const plan = await provider.chat(messages, {
              ...options,
              model: candidateModel,
            });
            this.config.groqModel = candidateModel;
            this.persistModelPreference('groqModel', candidateModel);
            return {
              response: plan,
              activeTier,
              provider: 'groq',
              modelUsed: candidateModel,
              fallbackNotice: `Auto-switched to Groq ${candidateModel} (${activeModel} was unavailable/limited)`,
            };
          } catch (nextErr) {
            console.warn(`[Vision AI Router] Groq fallback candidate ${candidateModel} failed:`, nextErr);
          }
        }
      } else if (provider.providerType === 'gemini') {
        const fallbacks =
          this.config.geminiFallbackModels && this.config.geminiFallbackModels.length > 0
            ? this.config.geminiFallbackModels
            : DEFAULT_GEMINI_FALLBACKS;

        for (const candidateModel of fallbacks) {
          if (candidateModel === activeModel) continue;
          try {
            console.log(`[Vision AI Router] Auto-switching to Gemini fallback model: ${candidateModel}`);
            const plan = await provider.chat(messages, {
              ...options,
              model: candidateModel,
            });
            this.config.geminiModel = candidateModel;
            this.persistModelPreference('geminiModel', candidateModel);
            return {
              response: plan,
              activeTier,
              provider: 'gemini',
              modelUsed: candidateModel,
              fallbackNotice: `Auto-switched to Gemini ${candidateModel} (${activeModel} was unavailable/limited)`,
            };
          } catch (nextErr) {
            console.warn(`[Vision AI Router] Gemini fallback candidate ${candidateModel} failed:`, nextErr);
          }
        }
      }

      // Strategy 2: Cross-provider failover
      const alternates: Array<{
        type: LLMProviderType;
        create: () => { provider: LLMProvider; model: string };
      }> = [];

      if (provider.providerType !== 'openrouter' && this.config.apiKeys.openrouter) {
        alternates.push({
          type: 'openrouter',
          create: () => {
            const m = this.config.openRouterModel ?? DEFAULT_OPENROUTER_FALLBACKS[0];
            return {
              provider: new OpenRouterAdapter(this.config.apiKeys.openrouter!, m),
              model: m,
            };
          },
        });
      }

      if (provider.providerType !== 'gemini' && this.config.apiKeys.gemini) {
        alternates.push({
          type: 'gemini',
          create: () => {
            const m = this.config.geminiModel ?? 'gemini-3.8-flash';
            return {
              provider: new GeminiAdapter(this.config.apiKeys.gemini!, m),
              model: m,
            };
          },
        });
      }

      if (provider.providerType !== 'groq' && this.config.apiKeys.groq) {
        alternates.push({
          type: 'groq',
          create: () => {
            const m = this.config.groqModel ?? 'llama-3.3-70b-versatile';
            return {
              provider: new GroqAdapter(this.config.apiKeys.groq!, m),
              model: m,
            };
          },
        });
      }

      for (const alt of alternates) {
        try {
          const { provider: altProvider, model: altModel } = alt.create();
          console.log(`[Vision AI Router] Cross-provider failover to ${alt.type} (${altModel})`);
          const plan = await altProvider.chat(messages, {
            ...options,
            model: altModel,
          });
          return {
            response: plan,
            activeTier,
            provider: alt.type,
            modelUsed: altModel,
            fallbackNotice: `Switched provider to ${alt.type} (${altModel}) due to usage limit`,
          };
        } catch (altErr) {
          console.warn(`[Vision AI Router] Alternate provider ${alt.type} failed:`, altErr);
        }
      }

      // Re-throw if all automated fallbacks fail
      throw err;
    }
  }

  async summarizePage(rawText: string): Promise<string> {
    const { provider } = this.getProviderForTier(0);
    if (provider instanceof OllamaAdapter) {
      return provider.summarizeAndScrubPII(rawText);
    }
    // Fallback to standard provider if Ollama is unavailable
    const prompt = `Scrub any sensitive PII (credit cards, phone numbers, passwords) and summarize this page in 3 sentences:\n\n${rawText.slice(
      0,
      10000
    )}`;
    const plan = await provider.chat([
      { role: 'system', content: 'You are a privacy-preserving text summarizer.' },
      { role: 'user', content: prompt },
    ]);
    return plan.thought;
  }
}

File: entrypoints/sidepanel/App.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { AgentSessionState, TabSessionSummary } from '../../src/lib/agent/types';
import { TokenTelemetryHUD } from './components/TokenTelemetryHUD';
import { ApiKeyVault } from './components/ApiKeyVault';
import { SkillsDrawer } from './components/SkillsDrawer';
import { ApprovalModal } from './components/ApprovalModal';
import { TaskStream } from './components/TaskStream';
import { TabSelectorBar } from './components/TabSelectorBar';
import { SiteSkill } from '../../src/lib/skills/skillStore';
import { KeyRound, Sparkles, Orbit, Globe, RefreshCw, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';

const INITIAL_STATE: AgentSessionState = {
  status: 'idle',
  goal: '',
  tabId: 0,
  stepNumber: 0,
  maxSteps: 25,
  currentThought: '',
  pendingActions: [],
  requiresApproval: false,
  tokenMetrics: {
    totalEstimatedTokens: 0,
    cachedPrefixTokens: 0,
    freshTokens: 0,
    estimatedCostDiscountPercent: 0,
  },
  completedTraces: [],
};

interface ActiveTabInfo {
  id?: number;
  title?: string;
  url?: string;
  isInternal?: boolean;
}

export default function App(): React.JSX.Element {
  const [state, setState] = useState<AgentSessionState>(INITIAL_STATE);
  const [selectedTabId, setSelectedTabId] = useState<number>(0);
  const [promptsByTab, setPromptsByTab] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [tabSessions, setTabSessions] = useState<TabSessionSummary[]>([]);
  const [vaultOpen, setVaultOpen] = useState(false);
  const [skillsOpen, setSkillsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ActiveTabInfo>({});

  const activePrompt = (selectedTabId > 0 ? promptsByTab[selectedTabId] : '') || '';

  const handlePromptChange = (val: string) => {
    if (selectedTabId > 0) {
      setPromptsByTab((prev) => ({ ...prev, [selectedTabId]: val }));
    }
  };

  const refreshTabSessions = () => {
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      try {
        chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'LIST_TAB_SESSIONS' }, (res) => {
          if (chrome.runtime.lastError) return;
          if (res?.sessions && Array.isArray(res.sessions)) {
            setTabSessions(res.sessions);
          }
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[Vision AI] refreshTabSessions error:', err);
      }
    }
  };

  const fetchTabState = (tabId: number) => {
    if (tabId <= 0) return;
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      try {
        chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_AGENT_STATE', tabId }, (res) => {
          if (chrome.runtime.lastError) return;
          if (res && typeof res === 'object') {
            setState(res as AgentSessionState);
          }
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[Vision AI] fetchTabState error:', err);
      }
    }
  };

  const refreshActiveTab = async () => {
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.tabs?.query) {
      try {
        const [tab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, lastFocusedWindow: true });
        if (tab && tab.id) {
          const url = tab.url || '';
          const isInternal =
            url.startsWith('chrome://') ||
            url.startsWith('chrome-extension://') ||
            url.startsWith('edge://') ||
            url.startsWith('devtools://') ||
            url.startsWith('about:');

          setActiveTab({
            id: tab.id,
            title: tab.title || (isInternal ? 'Chrome Internal Page' : 'Current Webpage'),
            url: tab.url,
            isInternal,
          });

          setSelectedTabId((prev) => {
            if (prev <= 0) {
              fetchTabState(tab.id!);
              return tab.id!;
            }
            return prev;
          });
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[Vision AI] Error querying active tab:', err);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleSelectTab = (tabId: number) => {
    setSelectedTabId(tabId);
    fetchTabState(tabId);
    const session = tabSessions.find((s) => s.tabId === tabId);
    if (session) {
      const isInternal =
        session.url.startsWith('chrome://') ||
        session.url.startsWith('chrome-extension://') ||
        session.url.startsWith('edge://') ||
        session.url.startsWith('devtools://') ||
        session.url.startsWith('about:');
      setActiveTab({
        id: session.tabId,
        title: session.title,
        url: session.url,
        isInternal,
      });
    }
  };

  const handleFocusChromeTab = (tabId: number) => {
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.tabs?.update) {
      chrome.tabs.update(tabId, { active: true }).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    // 1. Initial tab discovery & state fetch
    refreshActiveTab();
    refreshTabSessions();

    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'GET_AGENT_STATE' }, (res) => {
        if (chrome.runtime.lastError) return;
        if (res && typeof res === 'object') {
          setState(res as AgentSessionState);
          if (res.tabId && res.tabId > 0) {
            setSelectedTabId(res.tabId);
          }
        }
      });

      // 2. Real-time background broadcast listener
      const listener = (msg: { type: string; state?: AgentSessionState }) => {
        if (msg && msg.type === 'AGENT_STATE_UPDATED' && msg.state) {
          // Update tab session badges
          setTabSessions((prev) =>
            prev.map((s) =>
              s.tabId === msg.state!.tabId
                ? {
                    ...s,
                    status: msg.state!.status,
                    stepNumber: msg.state!.stepNumber,
                    goal: msg.state!.goal,
                  }
                : s
            )
          );

          // Only overwrite active workspace if message belongs to selected tab
          setSelectedTabId((currentSelected) => {
            if (msg.state!.tabId === currentSelected || currentSelected <= 0) {
              setState(msg.state!);
              return msg.state!.tabId;
            }
            return currentSelected;
          });
        }
      };

      chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener(listener);

      // 3. Browser tab event listeners
      const onTabActivated = (info: { tabId: number }) => {
        setSelectedTabId(info.tabId);
        fetchTabState(info.tabId);
        refreshActiveTab();
        refreshTabSessions();
      };

      const onTabUpdated = () => {
        refreshActiveTab();
        refreshTabSessions();
      };

      const onTabRemoved = () => {
        refreshTabSessions();
      };

      chrome.tabs?.onActivated?.addListener(onTabActivated);
      chrome.tabs?.onUpdated?.addListener(onTabUpdated);
      chrome.tabs?.onRemoved?.addListener(onTabRemoved);

      return () => {
        chrome.runtime.onMessage.removeListener(listener);
        chrome.tabs?.onActivated?.removeListener(onTabActivated);
        chrome.tabs?.onUpdated?.removeListener(onTabUpdated);
        chrome.tabs?.onRemoved?.removeListener(onTabRemoved);
      };
    }
  }, []);

  const handleStartTask = async (taskGoal?: string) => {
    const goalToRun = taskGoal ?? activePrompt;
    if (!goalToRun.trim()) return;

    const targetTabId = selectedTabId > 0 ? selectedTabId : activeTab.id;

    if (activeTab.isInternal) {
      setState((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        status: 'error',
        error:
          'Vision AI cannot automate internal Chrome pages (chrome://). Please open or switch to any website (e.g. google.com or wikipedia.org) and try again.',
      }));
      return;
    }

    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'START_TASK', goal: goalToRun, tabId: targetTabId }, () => {
        setState((prev) => ({
          ...prev,
          status: 'running',
          goal: goalToRun,
          stepNumber: 1,
          error: undefined,
        }));
        refreshTabSessions();
      });
    }
  };

  const handlePause = () => {
    const targetTabId = selectedTabId > 0 ? selectedTabId : undefined;
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'PAUSE_TASK', tabId: targetTabId });
    }
  };

  const handleResume = () => {
    const targetTabId = selectedTabId > 0 ? selectedTabId : undefined;
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'RESUME_TASK', tabId: targetTabId });
    }
  };

  const handleStop = () => {
    const targetTabId = selectedTabId > 0 ? selectedTabId : undefined;
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'STOP_TASK', tabId: targetTabId }, () => {
        setState((prev) => ({ ...prev, status: 'idle' }));
        refreshTabSessions();
      });
    }
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    const targetTabId = selectedTabId > 0 ? selectedTabId : undefined;
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'STOP_TASK', tabId: targetTabId }, () => {
        setState(INITIAL_STATE);
        if (selectedTabId > 0) {
          setPromptsByTab((prev) => ({ ...prev, [selectedTabId]: '' }));
        }
        refreshTabSessions();
      });
    } else {
      setState(INITIAL_STATE);
      if (selectedTabId > 0) {
        setPromptsByTab((prev) => ({ ...prev, [selectedTabId]: '' }));
      }
    }
  };

  const handleApprove = () => {
    const targetTabId = selectedTabId > 0 ? selectedTabId : undefined;
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'APPROVE_ACTION', approved: true, tabId: targetTabId });
    }
  };

  const handleReject = () => {
    const targetTabId = selectedTabId > 0 ? selectedTabId : undefined;
    if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.runtime?.sendMessage) {
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'APPROVE_ACTION', approved: false, tabId: targetTabId });
    }
  };

  const handleRunSkill = (skill: SiteSkill) => {
    if (selectedTabId > 0) {
      setPromptsByTab((prev) => ({ ...prev, [selectedTabId]: skill.goalPattern }));
    }
    handleStartTask(skill.goalPattern);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-screen w-full bg-orbit-dark text-slate-100 p-3.5 select-none font-sans overflow-hidden">
      {/* Top Header */}
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-orbit-border pb-3 mb-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center">
            <Orbit className="w-5 h-5 text-orbit-neon animate-spin" style={{ animationDuration: '8s' }} />
            <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-orbit-neon absolute" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-sm font-black tracking-tight flex items-center gap-1.5">
              Vision AI
              <span className="text-[9px] bg-slate-800 text-orbit-accent border border-slate-700 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-mono font-normal">
                v0.1
              </span>
            </h1>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <button
            onClick={() => setSkillsOpen(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700/80 hover:border-slate-600 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
            title="Saved 0-Token Domain Skills"
          >
            <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold">Skills</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setVaultOpen(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700/80 hover:border-slate-600 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
            title="API Keys Vault"
          >
            <KeyRound className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orbit-accent" />
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold">Vault</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Target Tab Selector & Active Agents Bar */}
      <TabSelectorBar
        sessions={tabSessions}
        selectedTabId={selectedTabId}
        onSelectTab={handleSelectTab}
        onFocusChromeTab={handleFocusChromeTab}
        onRefreshTabs={refreshTabSessions}
      />

      {/* Target Webpage Status Bar */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 mb-2.5 text-[11px] shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0 flex-1">
          <span
            className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0 ${
              activeTab.isInternal
                ? 'bg-amber-400 animate-pulse'
                : activeTab.url
                ? 'bg-emerald-400'
                : 'bg-slate-500'
            }`}
          />
          <Globe className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
          <span className="text-slate-400 shrink-0 font-medium">Page:</span>
          <span
            className="text-slate-200 truncate font-mono text-[10.5px]"
            title={activeTab.url || 'No active tab detected'}
          >
            {activeTab.title || (activeTab.url ? activeTab.url : 'Detecting tab...')}
          </span>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={refreshActiveTab}
          title="Refresh active tab connection"
          className="text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-300 ml-2 p-0.5 rounded transition-colors shrink-0"
        >
          <RefreshCw className="w-3 h-3" />
        </button>
      </div>

      {activeTab.isInternal && (
        <div className="flex items-start gap-1.5 bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-600/30 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 mb-2.5 text-[11px] text-amber-300">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <span>
            Target is a Chrome internal page (<code>{activeTab.url?.slice(0, 20)}...</code>). Switch your browser tab to a website like{' '}
            <strong className="text-amber-200">google.com</strong> or <strong className="text-amber-200">wikipedia.org</strong> to automate.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Token Telemetry HUD */}
      <div className="mb-3">
        <TokenTelemetryHUD
          metrics={state.tokenMetrics}
          activeTier={state.activeTier}
          activeProvider={state.activeProvider}
          activeModel={state.activeModel}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Interactive Task Stream & Controls */}
      <TaskStream
        state={state}
        prompt={activePrompt}
        onPromptChange={handlePromptChange}
        onStart={() => handleStartTask()}
        onPause={handlePause}
        onResume={handleResume}
        onStop={handleStop}
        onReset={handleReset}
      />

      {/* Human-in-the-Loop Intercept Modal */}
      {state.requiresApproval && (
        <ApprovalModal
          action={state.approvalDetails?.action}
          reason={state.approvalDetails?.reason}
          onApprove={handleApprove}
          onReject={handleReject}
        />
      )}

      {/* Drawers / Modals */}
      <ApiKeyVault isOpen={vaultOpen} onClose={() => setVaultOpen(false)} />
      <SkillsDrawer
        isOpen={skillsOpen}
        onClose={() => setSkillsOpen(false)}
        onRunSkill={handleRunSkill}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: entrypoints/background.ts

import { defineBackground } from 'wxt/sandbox';
import { LLMRouter } from '../src/lib/llm/router';
import { AgentStateMachine } from '../src/lib/agent/stateMachine';
import { ExtensionMessage } from '../src/lib/agent/types';
import { globalSkillStore, compileTraceToSkill } from '../src/lib/skills/skillStore';
import { classifyActionSafety } from '../src/lib/safety/interceptor';

export default defineBackground(() => {
  console.log('[Vision AI] Service Worker background loop initialized.');

  // Automatically open the side panel when the toolbar action icon is clicked
  if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined') {
    if (chrome.sidePanel?.setOptions) {
      chrome.sidePanel.setOptions({ path: 'sidepanel.html', enabled: true }).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (chrome.sidePanel?.setPanelBehavior) {
      chrome.sidePanel
        .setPanelBehavior({ openPanelOnActionClick: true })
        .catch((err) => console.warn('[Vision AI] Failed to set panel behavior:', err));
    }

    chrome.runtime?.onInstalled?.addListener(() => {
      if (chrome.sidePanel?.setOptions) {
        chrome.sidePanel.setOptions({ path: 'sidepanel.html', enabled: true }).catch(() => {});
      }
      if (chrome.sidePanel?.setPanelBehavior) {
        chrome.sidePanel
          .setPanelBehavior({ openPanelOnActionClick: true })
          .catch((err) => console.warn('[Vision AI] onInstalled setPanelBehavior error:', err));
      }
    });

    if (chrome.action?.onClicked) {
      chrome.action.onClicked.addListener(async (tab) => {
        try {
          if (chrome.sidePanel?.open) {
            if (tab.windowId) {
              await chrome.sidePanel.open({ windowId: tab.windowId });
            } else if (tab.id) {
              await chrome.sidePanel.open({ tabId: tab.id });
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('[Vision AI] chrome.sidePanel.open error:', err);
        }
      });
    }
  }

  // Default initial router configuration
  const router = new LLMRouter({
    apiKeys: {},
  });

  // Synchronize stored API keys and routing configuration from chrome.storage.local
  const syncStorageToRouter = async (): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      const data = await chrome.storage.local.get(['apiKeys', 'modelRoutingConfig']);
      if (data.apiKeys) {
        router.updateConfig({ apiKeys: data.apiKeys });
      }
      if (data.modelRoutingConfig) {
        router.updateConfig(data.modelRoutingConfig);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[Vision AI] syncStorageToRouter error:', err);
    }
  };

  // Initial load
  syncStorageToRouter();

  // Keep live router in sync with any changes in ApiKeyVault / model preferences
  chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener((changes, area) => {
    if (area === 'local') {
      if (changes.apiKeys) {
        router.updateConfig({ apiKeys: changes.apiKeys.newValue });
      }
      if (changes.modelRoutingConfig) {
        router.updateConfig(changes.modelRoutingConfig.newValue);
      }
    }
  });

  // Multi-Tab Agent Manager (Dassi AI style per-tab isolation)
  const tabAgents = new Map<number, AgentStateMachine>();

  const createAgentHooks = (boundTabId: number) => ({
    checkSkillCache: async (url: string, goal: string) => {
      const skill = await globalSkillStore.findMatchingSkill(url, goal);
      if (!skill) return { matched: false };
      await globalSkillStore.recordSkillExecution(skill.id);
      return {
        matched: true,
        actions: skill.steps.map((s) => ({
          type: s.action,
          value: s.value,
          stop_if_url_changes: s.stop_if_url_changes,
        })),
      };
    },
    isDestructiveAction: (action: any, text?: string) => {
      const res = classifyActionSafety(action, text || '', '');
      return {
        destructive: res.isDestructive && res.severity !== 'low',
        reason: res.reason,
      };
    },
    onStateChange: (state: any) => {
      // Broadcast state updates to open sidepanel UI
      chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: 'AGENT_STATE_UPDATED', state }).catch(() => {
        // Suppress errors when sidepanel is not open
      });

      if (state.status === 'completed' && state.completedTraces.length >= 1) {
        const targetTabId = state.tabId || boundTabId;
        const recordSkill = (tabUrl: string) => {
          compileTraceToSkill(tabUrl, state.goal, state.completedTraces, router).catch((err) => {
            console.warn('[Vision AI] Failed to compile trace to skill:', err);
          });
        };

        if (targetTabId && typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.tabs?.get) {
          chrome.tabs
            .get(targetTabId)
            .then((tab) => recordSkill(tab?.url || 'https://example.com'))
            .catch(() => recordSkill('https://example.com'));
        } else {
          recordSkill('https://example.com');
        }
      }
    },
  });

  const getOrCreateAgentForTab = (tabId: number): AgentStateMachine => {
    let agent = tabAgents.get(tabId);
    if (!agent) {
      const storageKey = tabId > 0 ? `orbit_agent_session_tab_${tabId}` : undefined;
      agent = new AgentStateMachine(router, createAgentHooks(tabId), storageKey);
      tabAgents.set(tabId, agent);
      if (storageKey) {
        agent.restoreSession().catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    return agent;
  };

  const resolveTargetTabId = async (requestedTabId?: number): Promise<number> => {
    if (requestedTabId && requestedTabId > 0) return requestedTabId;
    if (typeof chrome === 'undefined' || !chrome.tabs?.query) return 0;
    try {
      const [lastFocused] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, lastFocusedWindow: true });
      if (lastFocused?.id) return lastFocused.id;
    } catch {}
    try {
      const [activeTab] = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true, currentWindow: true });
      if (activeTab?.id) return activeTab.id;
    } catch {}
    try {
      const allTabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ active: true });
      if (allTabs[0]?.id) return allTabs[0].id;
    } catch {}
    return 0;
  };

  // Restore sessions for currently open tabs on background reload
  if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.tabs?.query) {
    chrome.tabs.query({}).then((tabs) => {
      for (const t of tabs) {
        if (t.id) {
          getOrCreateAgentForTab(t.id);
        }
      }
    }).catch(() => {});
  }

  // Clean up tab agent when tab is closed
  if (typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.tabs?.onRemoved) {
    chrome.tabs.onRemoved.addListener((tabId) => {
      const agent = tabAgents.get(tabId);
      if (agent) {
        agent.stop().catch(() => {});
        tabAgents.delete(tabId);
      }
    });
  }

  chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((message: ExtensionMessage, _sender, sendResponse) => {
    if (!message || typeof message !== 'object') return false;

    switch (message.type) {
      case 'START_TASK': {
        resolveTargetTabId(message.tabId).then(async (tabId) => {
          await syncStorageToRouter();
          const agent = getOrCreateAgentForTab(tabId);
          await agent.startTask(message.goal, tabId);
          sendResponse({ success: true, tabId });
        });
        return true;
      }

      case 'PAUSE_TASK': {
        resolveTargetTabId(message.tabId).then(async (tabId) => {
          const agent = getOrCreateAgentForTab(tabId);
          await agent.pause();
          sendResponse({ success: true, tabId });
        });
        return true;
      }

      case 'RESUME_TASK': {
        resolveTargetTabId(message.tabId).then(async (tabId) => {
          await syncStorageToRouter();
          const agent = getOrCreateAgentForTab(tabId);
          await agent.resume();
          sendResponse({ success: true, tabId });
        });
        return true;
      }

      case 'STOP_TASK': {
        resolveTargetTabId(message.tabId).then(async (tabId) => {
          const agent = getOrCreateAgentForTab(tabId);
          await agent.stop();
          sendResponse({ success: true, tabId });
        });
        return true;
      }

      case 'GET_AGENT_STATE': {
        resolveTargetTabId(message.tabId).then((tabId) => {
          const agent = getOrCreateAgentForTab(tabId);
          sendResponse(agent.getState());
        });
        return true;
      }

      case 'APPROVE_ACTION': {
        resolveTargetTabId(message.tabId).then(async (tabId) => {
          const agent = getOrCreateAgentForTab(tabId);
          await agent.approvePendingAction(message.approved);
          sendResponse({ success: true, tabId });
        });
        return true;
      }

      case 'LIST_TAB_SESSIONS': {
        (async () => {
          try {
            const allTabs = typeof chrome !== 'undefined' && chrome.tabs?.query ? await chrome.tabs.query({}) : [];
            const sessions = allTabs
              .filter((tab) => typeof tab.id === 'number')
              .map((tab) => {
                const agent = tabAgents.get(tab.id!);
                const state = agent ? agent.getState() : null;
                return {
                  tabId: tab.id!,
                  title: tab.title || 'Untitled Tab',
                  url: tab.url || '',
                  status: state ? state.status : 'idle',
                  stepNumber: state ? state.stepNumber : 0,
                  goal: state ? state.goal : '',
                };
              });
            sendResponse({ sessions });
          } catch (err) {
            sendResponse({ sessions: [], error: String(err) });
          }
        })();
        return true;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }

    return false;
  });
});

# LOOP_STATE.md — Loop Engineering Protocol State

## Current Active Task
- **Task ID:** `M8.2`
- **Status:** `[x]` Completed
- **Completed At:** 2026-10-17
- **Goal:** Side Panel Per-Tab Workspace & Live Tab Switcher UI (`entrypoints/sidepanel/App.tsx`).

---

## Milestone Backlog

### Milestone 0: Harness & Loop Setup
- [x] `M0.1` Scaffold WXT + React + TypeScript + Tailwind + Vitest + `fake-indexeddb` + `happy-dom`.
- [x] `M0.2` Create `AGENTS.md`, `LOOP_STATE.md`, and `.agent/skills/token-budget/SKILL.md` defining the 800-token DOM distillation cap and byte-stable caching rules.

### Milestone 1: Multi-Provider Router & Byte-Stable Context Manager
- [x] `M1.1` Implement `LLMRouter` with adapters for Anthropic, Gemini, Groq, OpenRouter, and Ollama.
- [x] `M1.2` Implement `ByteStableContextManager` ensuring prefix immutability across steps + token estimator.
- [x] `M1.3` Write Vitest suite verifying Anthropic `cache_control` placement, tiered routing selection, and byte-identical prefixes across 5 simulated steps.

### Milestone 2: DOM Distiller & Mutation Delta Engine
- [x] `M2.1` Build `distiller.ts` with numeric `[ID]` tagging, visibility filtering, and `WeakRef` registry.
- [x] `M2.2` Build `delta.ts` using `MutationObserver` to emit compact `ADDED / MODIFIED / REMOVED` diffs.
- [x] `M2.3` Write Vitest benchmark feeding a complex 1,000-node mock e-commerce/GitHub DOM and asserting output is under 800 tokens and diffs are under 100 tokens.

### Milestone 3: CDP Hardware Actuator, Macro-Batch Runner & SoM Vision
- [x] `M3.1` Implement `cdp/actuator.ts` (`chrome.debugger` mouse/keyboard dispatch) + synthetic fallback.
- [x] `M3.2` Implement speculative macro-batch executor with mid-batch validation/URL-change circuit breakers.
- [x] `M3.3` Implement `som.ts` canvas bounding-box renderer and cropped WebP extractor.

### Milestone 4: Stateful Background Agent Loop & IndexedDB Skill Compiler
- [x] `M4.1` Build the Service Worker state machine (`Observe -> Check Skill Cache -> Route LLM -> Execute Macro-Batch -> Diff`).
- [x] `M4.2` Implement `IndexedDB` Skill Store and post-run Tier-2 skill compilation.
- [x] `M4.3` Add Human-in-the-Loop safety interceptor for destructive actions (`submit`, `delete`, `purchase`, `send`).

### Milestone 5: Side Panel UI & Live Browser Verification
- [x] `M5.1` Build React Side Panel UI: Chat/Task stream, Provider API Key Vault, Tier Router selector, Live Token Savings Counter (Cached vs. Uncached tokens), and Saved Skills drawer.
- [x] `M5.2` Run full WXT build and verify UI rendering and DOM distillation in Antigravity's built-in Chrome browser, generating final Verification Artifacts.

### Milestone 6: Wire Runtime Entrypoints & Fix MV3 Bugs
- [x] `M6.1` Fix `entrypoints/content.ts` Frame Race (`allFrames: false` + `PING` case) & Add Visual Spotlight (`highlightTarget` + `selector`/`elementText` in `ActionTrace`).
- [x] `M6.2` Fix Service Worker Cold-Start Race & Wire Hooks in `entrypoints/background.ts` (`syncStorageToRouter`, `checkSkillCache`, `isDestructiveAction`, `compileTraceToSkill`).
- [x] `M6.3` Fix DOM Distiller Slot Exhaustion & Add Page Text Summary (`src/lib/dom/distiller.ts`).
- [x] `M6.4` Add `navigate` & `go_back` Actions + OpenRouter HTTP 400 Retry.

### Milestone 7: Coursera, Cisco NetAcad & Complex SPA/LMS Engine
- [x] `M7.1` Deep Shadow DOM Traversal & LMS Priority Element Reservation (`src/lib/dom/distiller.ts`, `tests/dom-distiller.test.ts`).
- [x] `M7.2` Inner Scrollable Container Engine, PointerEvents & Video Control (`src/lib/executor/macroExecutor.ts`, `src/lib/llm/types.ts`, `src/lib/llm/parser.ts`, `src/lib/llm/contextManager.ts`, `tests/actuator-and-vision.test.ts`).
- [x] `M7.3` Cross-Frame Execution Fallback for NetAcad SCORM Iframes & Completion Heuristic Fix (`src/lib/agent/stateMachine.ts`).

### Milestone 8: Multi-Tab Individual Agent Sessions (Dassi-Style Per-Tab Isolation)
- [x] `M8.1` Multi-Tab Session Manager (`entrypoints/background.ts`, `src/lib/agent/stateMachine.ts`, `src/lib/agent/types.ts`, `tests/parser-and-loop.test.ts`).
- [x] `M8.2` Side Panel Per-Tab Workspace & Live Tab Switcher UI (`entrypoints/sidepanel/App.tsx`).

## Current Status
Milestone 7 & Milestone 8 completed and verified across all quality gates!
- Coursera & Cisco NetAcad complex LMS automation engine fully equipped with deep Shadow DOM walker, smart inner-container scrolling, composed pointer events, SCORM child frame discovery & execution, and HTML5 video automation.
- Multi-Tab Isolated Agent Sessions (Dassi AI style) fully operational with per-tab state machines, isolated session storage keys, and live TabSelectorBar workspace switching with background agent badges.
